# CS5242 Neural Networks and Deep Learning 复习笔记 · Review Notes

## 开始之前：零基础词汇表 · Before You Start: Glossary

深度学习一句话：**用一个带参数的大函数 f(x; w) 去拟合数据，用「往下坡走」（梯度下降）的办法把参数 w 调到误差最小**。这门课前六讲就是把这句话拆开讲清楚。下面的词先看一遍。

*Deep learning in one sentence: fit the data with a big parametrised function f(x; w), and tune the parameters w to minimise the error by repeatedly walking downhill (gradient descent). The first six lectures unpack this sentence. Read the terms below first.*

| 词 · Term | 大白话 | In plain English | 例子 · Example |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 样本 / 特征 / 标签 · sample / feature / label | 样本是一条数据；特征 x 是它的输入属性；标签 y 是我们想预测的答案 | A sample is one data point; the feature x is its input attribute; the label y is the answer we want to predict | 一个人：x = 身高，y = 体重 · a person: x = height, y = weight |
| 向量 · vector | 一列数，课上默认是「竖着的」列向量，粗体 x | A list of numbers; the course uses column vectors by default, written in bold x | 房子的 (面积, 楼层, 房龄) · a house's (size, floor, age) |
| 矩阵 · matrix | 一张数表；课上常把每个样本放成矩阵 X 的一行 | A table of numbers; each sample is usually one row of X | m 个样本 × n 个特征 · m samples × n features |
| 点积 · dot product wᵀx | 对应位置相乘再加起来，就是「加权求和」 | Multiply matching entries and add them up: a weighted sum | (1,2)·(3,4) = 3 + 8 = 11 |
| 参数 / 权重 · parameters / weights w, b | 模型里可以调的旋钮；w 是斜率/权重，b 是截距/偏置 | The tunable knobs of the model; w is the slope or weight, b is the intercept or bias | ỹ = wx + b |
| 超参数 · hyperparameter | 不是训练学出来、而是人预先定的设置 | A setting chosen by people beforehand, not learned in training | 学习率、层数、batch size、多项式次数 · learning rate, depth, batch size, polynomial degree |
| 损失函数 · loss function | 衡量预测和真值差多少，越小越好 | Measures how far the prediction is from the truth; smaller is better | 平方误差 · squared error (ỹ − y)² |
| 导数 · derivative | 旋钮微微一拧，输出变多少；也就是曲线的斜率 | How much the output changes when you nudge a knob: the slope of the curve | f(x) = x² 的导数是 2x · the derivative of x² is 2x |
| 偏导数 / 梯度 · partial derivative / gradient ∇ | 多个旋钮时，对每个单独求导，排成一个向量就是梯度；它指向「上坡最陡」的方向 | With many knobs, differentiate with respect to each one and stack the results: that vector is the gradient, pointing in the steepest uphill direction | ∇f = (∂f/∂w₁, ∂f/∂w₂) |
| 链式法则 · chain rule | 复合函数求导：外层导数 × 内层导数。反向传播就是反复用链式法则 | Derivative of a composition = outer derivative × inner derivative. Backpropagation is the chain rule applied over and over | ln(x + x²) 的导数 = (1 + 2x)/(x + x²) |
| 学习率 · learning rate α or η | 每一步下坡走多远；太大会来回跳甚至发散，太小走得慢 | How far each downhill step goes; too large oscillates or diverges, too small is slow | 0.1、0.01 |
| 前向传播 / 反向传播 · forward / backward pass | 前向：由输入算出预测和损失；反向：从损失往回算每个参数的梯度 | Forward: from the input to the prediction and loss. Backward: from the loss back to the gradient of every parameter | 先 forward 再 backward · forward first, then backward |
| 训练 vs 推理 · training vs inference | 训练要前向 + 反向 + 更新参数；推理（使用模型）只要前向 | Training = forward + backward + update; inference (using the model) = forward only | 用 ChatGPT 就是推理 · using ChatGPT is inference |
| 训练集 / 验证集 / 测试集 · train / validation / test set | 训练集学参数；验证集调超参数；测试集只在最后报告成绩，绝不能用来训练或调参 | Train set learns parameters; validation set tunes hyperparameters; test set only reports the final score and is never used for training or tuning | 像考试题不能提前看 · like not seeing the exam beforehand |
| 过拟合 / 欠拟合 · overfitting / underfitting | 过拟合：把训练数据背得太死，新数据表现差；欠拟合：模型太简单，连训练数据都拟合不好 | Overfitting: memorising the training data and doing badly on new data. Underfitting: a model too simple to fit even the training data | 用 9 次多项式拟合 10 个点 · a degree-9 polynomial through 10 points |
| 回归 vs 分类 · regression vs classification | 回归预测连续数值；分类预测类别 | Regression predicts a continuous number; classification predicts a category | 明天气温 vs 明天是否下雨 · tomorrow's temperature vs whether it rains |
| 概率 / 期望 · probability / expectation E\[·\] | 概率在 0 到 1 之间；期望是加权平均 | A probability lies between 0 and 1; an expectation is a probability-weighted average | 掷骰子期望 3.5 · a fair die has expectation 3.5 |
| log | 本课里一般是自然对数 ln；log 把乘法变成加法，用在交叉熵里 | Usually the natural log ln in this course; it turns products into sums, which is why it appears in cross-entropy | log(ab) = log a + log b |

**读笔记的建议**：本课所有内容都套在同一个流程里——**准备数据 → 选模型 → 选损失函数 → 用梯度下降训练 → 在验证/测试集上评估 → 部署**。每讲只是换了其中一两个环节。看到公式先问：它属于哪个环节？

*How to read these notes: everything in the course fits one pipeline — prepare data → choose a model → choose a loss → train with gradient descent → evaluate on validation/test data → deploy. Each lecture changes only one or two of these steps. When you see a formula, first ask which step it belongs to.*

## 课程地图 · Course Map

全课分两半：前六讲（L1–L6）从「什么是深度学习」走到「怎么把一个多层网络训练好」；后四讲（L7–L10）讲 Transformer 和大语言模型（BERT、GPT 与推理）。课件主要是 L1、L4、L5、L6 和 L7–L10 的幻灯片；L2、L3 以讲义和手写白板为主（白板是图片，本笔记用讲义和 quiz 补全）；L10 课件是 L9 的扩充版。

*The course has two halves. L1–L6 go from "what is deep learning" to "how to train a multi-layer network well"; L7–L10 cover the Transformer and large language models (BERT, GPT and inference). Most material comes from the slides of L1, L4–L6 and L7–L10; L2 and L3 are mainly lecture notes and handwritten whiteboards (the whiteboards are images, so these notes fill them in from the lecture notes and quizzes). The L10 slides are an extended version of L9.*

| 讲 · Lecture | 主题 · Topic | 一句话 · In one line | 必会内容 · Must know | 对应作业 · Assessment |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| L1 | 导论 · Introduction | 深度学习 = 自动学特征的多层函数 · deep learning = multi-layer functions that learn their own features | AI / ML / DL 的关系，传统 ML vs DL，发展历史 · AI vs ML vs DL, classical ML vs DL, history | — |
| L2 | 机器学习基础与泛化 · ML basics and generalisation | 训练误差小不等于测试误差小 · low training error does not mean low test error | 偏差与伪相关、训练 vs 推理、经验风险最小化、泛化误差、PAC、VC 维 · bias and spurious correlation, training vs inference, ERM, generalisation error, PAC, VC dimension | A1 Task 4 |
| L3 | 训练算法基础 · Training algorithm basics | 梯度下降及其变体 · gradient descent and its variants | GD、SGD、mini-batch、学习率与收敛/发散、动量、零阶方法 · GD, SGD, mini-batch, learning rate and convergence/divergence, momentum, zeroth-order methods | A2 |
| L4 | 线性回归 · Linear regression | 最简单的「模型 + 损失 + 梯度下降」 · the simplest model + loss + gradient descent | 单/多变量线性回归、平方损失、求导法则、向量化梯度 · uni/multivariate regression, squared loss, derivative rules, vectorised gradient | A2 |
| L5 | 感知机、回归与分类 · Perceptrons, regression and classification | 从一个神经元到多层网络，从回归到分类 · from one neuron to a network, from regression to classification | 感知机、MLP、反向传播、过/欠拟合、数据划分、逻辑回归、交叉熵、softmax、显存估算 · perceptron, MLP, backprop, over/underfitting, data splits, logistic regression, cross-entropy, softmax, memory estimates | A1 Task 1, A3 |
| L6 | 多层感知机与训练技巧 · MLPs and training tricks | 把深网络训练好的一整套办法 · the toolkit for training deep networks well | 激活函数、XOR、SGD、动量、RMSprop、Adam、学习率衰减、初始化、归一化、正则化 · activations, XOR, SGD, momentum, RMSprop, Adam, LR decay, initialisation, normalisation, regularisation | A1 Task 2, A4, A5 |
| L7 | Transformer 与 MoE · Transformer and MoE | 注意力让每个 token 一步看到全部，整句并行；MoE 参数大、计算不变 · attention lets every token see all tokens in one step, in parallel; MoE adds parameters without adding compute | QKV 注意力、√d\_k 缩放、因果掩码、多头、残差与 LayerNorm、12d² 参数、6ND 算力、MFU、门控与负载均衡 · QKV attention, √d\_k scaling, causal mask, multi-head, residual and LayerNorm, 12d² parameters, 6ND compute, MFU, gating and load balancing | Quiz 7 |
| L8 | BERT | 只用编码器，遮词猜词预训练一次，再加小头微调 · encoder only, pre-trained once by guessing masked words, then fine-tuned with a small head | 四个问题、MLM 与 80/10/10、NSP、输入表示、位置编码、warm-up、微调头、参数与算力 · the four questions, MLM and 80/10/10, NSP, input representation, positional encoding, warm-up, fine-tuning heads, parameters and compute | Quiz 8 |
| L9 | GPT-1、GPT-2、GPT-3 | 同一个损失放大 1500 倍：微调 → 零样本 → 少样本 · one loss scaled up 1,500×: fine-tuning → zero-shot → few-shot | N ≈ 12Ld²、6ND、PF-day、权重绑定、BPE、困惑度、前向次数、Pre-LN、上下文学习、GPT-3 的 Adam 设置 · N ≈ 12Ld², 6ND, PF-day, weight tying, BPE, perplexity, forward-pass counts, Pre-LN, in-context learning, GPT-3's Adam settings | Quiz 9, A5 |
| L10 | GPT 推理 · GPT inference | 模型只给概率，选词是另外的规则 · the model only gives probabilities; picking a token is a separate rule | prefill 与 decode、KV 缓存、贪心 / 采样 / top-k / beam search、长度惩罚 · prefill and decode, KV cache, greedy / sampling / top-k / beam search, length penalty | Quiz 10 |

一条主线串起来：**L4 线性回归**（一个神经元、没有激活）→ **L5 加上 sigmoid / softmax 做分类**，把神经元堆成 MLP，用反向传播求梯度 → **L6 换更好的激活和优化器**、用正则化防过拟合。L2 告诉你「为什么要关心测试误差」，L3 告诉你「梯度下降为什么能工作、何时会崩」。后半程：**L7 Transformer**（注意力 + FFN，MoE 把 FFN 换成专家）→ **L8 BERT**（只用编码器，遮词预训练 + 微调）→ **L9 GPT-1/2/3**（只用解码器，预测下一个词，越放越大）→ **L10 推理**（prefill/decode、KV 缓存、怎么从概率里选词）。从 L8 起每个模型都用同一个模板拆：架构、损失、数据、优化器。

*The storyline: L4 linear regression (one neuron, no activation) → L5 adds sigmoid/softmax for classification, stacks neurons into an MLP and gets gradients by backpropagation → L6 uses better activations and optimisers and regularises against overfitting. L2 explains why test error is what matters; L3 explains why gradient descent works and when it breaks. Second half: L7 Transformer (attention + FFN; MoE replaces the FFN with experts) → L8 BERT (encoder only, masked-word pre-training + fine-tuning) → L9 GPT-1/2/3 (decoder only, next-token prediction, scaled up) → L10 inference (prefill/decode, KV cache, choosing a token from probabilities). From L8 on, every model is taken apart with one template: architecture, loss, data, optimiser.*

## 逐讲笔记 · Lecture-by-Lecture Notes

### L1 导论：什么是深度学习 · Introduction: What Is Deep Learning

**这一讲在干什么**：给整门课定位。L1 基本只考概念（判断题、简答题），不考计算。读完你要能用自己的话说清三件事：AI、ML、DL 是什么关系；传统机器学习和深度学习差在哪；深度学习为什么最近十几年才火起来。

*What this lecture is about: it sets the scene for the course. L1 is examined only through concepts (true/false and short answers), not calculations. You should be able to explain three things in your own words: how AI, ML and DL relate; how classical machine learning differs from deep learning; and why deep learning took off only in the last decade or so.*

**1. AI ⊃ ML ⊃ DL：一层套一层 · Nested fields**

- **人工智能 AI**（1950 年代起）：让机器表现出人类的智能。早期的做法是「专家系统」：人把规则一条一条写进程序，比如「体温 > 38℃ 且咳嗽 → 可能感冒」。今天常见的 AI 大多是「窄 AI」，只会一件事：图像识别、机器翻译、推荐系统。

  *Artificial intelligence (from the 1950s): machines exhibiting human intelligence. Early systems were expert systems, with rules written by hand, e.g. "temperature > 38 °C and cough → possibly flu". Most AI today is narrow AI that does one thing: image recognition, machine translation, recommendation.*
- **机器学习 ML**（1980 年代起）：不再手写规则，而是让程序从数据（经验）里自己找规律。例子：要识别照片里有没有飞机，手写规则几乎不可能（颜色、角度、大小千变万化），但给程序一万张标好「有/没有飞机」的照片，它可以自己学出来。

  *Machine learning (from the 1980s): instead of hand-written rules, the program finds patterns in data (experience) by itself. Writing rules to spot an aeroplane in a photo is nearly impossible because colour, angle and size vary endlessly, but a program shown ten thousand labelled photos can learn it.*
- **深度学习 DL**：机器学习的一个分支，用「很多层非线性处理单元串起来」来提取和变换特征。数学上就是函数的复合 y = f₃(f₂(f₁(x)))，每一层 fᵢ 有自己的参数；「深」指的是层数多。

  *Deep learning: a branch of ML that uses a cascade of many layers of non-linear processing units to extract and transform features. Mathematically it is a composition of functions y = f₃(f₂(f₁(x))), each layer with its own parameters; "deep" means many layers.*
- 三者关系：AI ⊃ ML ⊃ DL。所有深度学习都是机器学习，反过来不成立（决策树、SVM 是机器学习但不是深度学习）。

  *AI ⊃ ML ⊃ DL. All deep learning is machine learning, but not the other way round (decision trees and SVMs are ML but not DL).*

**2. 传统机器学习 vs 深度学习（本讲最重要的一张图） · Classical ML vs deep learning (the key slide)**

任务：判断一张图片是不是猫。

*Task: decide whether an image shows a cat.*

- **传统机器学习分两步**：

  *Classical ML works in two steps:*
  1. 特征提取器 φ(x)：由人设计，比如边缘、颜色直方图、纹理统计，把原始像素 x 变成一串数字 φ(x)。这一步**不学习**，全靠人的领域知识。

     *Feature extractor φ(x): designed by people (edges, colour histograms, texture statistics) to turn raw pixels x into a vector φ(x). This step is not learned; it relies entirely on domain knowledge.*
  2. 分类器 wᵀφ(x)：比如结果 > 0 就判为猫。**只有 w 是从数据里学出来的**。

     *Classifier wᵀφ(x): e.g. predict "cat" if it is > 0. Only w is learned from data.*
  - 缺点：特征设计得不好，分类器再强也没用；换个任务（猫 → 汽车）要重新设计特征。

    *Drawback: with poor features no classifier can help, and a new task (cats → cars) needs new hand-designed features.*
- **深度学习一步到位**：特征提取和分类合成一个网络 f(x, w)，从原始像素直接输出答案。网络前几层会自动学出「边缘 → 纹理 → 部件（耳朵、眼睛）→ 整只猫」这样逐层变抽象的特征。特征和分类器一起从数据里学出来，这叫**特征学习**（feature learning，也叫表示学习）。

  *Deep learning does it in one go: feature extraction and classification are merged into one network f(x, w) that maps raw pixels straight to the answer. The early layers learn increasingly abstract features (edges → textures → parts such as ears and eyes → the whole cat). Features and classifier are learned together from data: this is feature (representation) learning.*
- 一句话记：传统 ML 只学「怎么用特征」，DL 连「用什么特征」也一起学。

  *In one line: classical ML learns only how to use the features; DL also learns which features to use.*

| 对比 · Aspect | 传统机器学习 · Classical ML | 深度学习 · Deep learning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 特征从哪来 · Where features come from | 人工设计 φ(x) · hand-designed φ(x) | 网络自动学 · learned by the network |
| 学什么 · What is learned | 只学分类器 w · only the classifier w | 特征 + 分类器一起学（端到端） · features and classifier together (end to end) |
| 对数据量的需求 · Data needed | 较少 · less | 很多，而且数据越多越好 · a lot, and more keeps helping |
| 换一个任务 · New task | 要重新设计特征 · redesign the features | 换数据重新训练即可 · retrain on new data |

**3. 为什么深度学习越来越重要 · Why deep learning matters more and more**

- 课件的逻辑：我们要的是从「数据」到「有用的信息」。过去中间那步「数据处理」要靠人；深度学习把它自动化了。不同应用再结合各自的领域知识（先验），设计出不同的新方法。

  *The slides' logic: we want to go from data to useful information. The data-processing step in between used to be done by people; deep learning automates it, and each application adds its own domain knowledge (priors) to design new methods.*
- **为什么是机器学习**：数据量和算力都在快速增长。

  *Why ML: data and computation are growing fast.*
- **为什么是深度学习**：

  *Why DL:*
  1. 少靠手工、多从数据学（特征学习），所以数据和算力越多，深度学习越占便宜。课件那张「性能 vs 数据量」的图：传统方法很快就饱和，结构固定的神经网络会随数据增加持续变好。

     *Less hand-crafting and more learning from data (feature learning), so DL benefits most from growth in data and compute. The "performance vs data" plot: classical methods saturate quickly, while a fixed neural architecture keeps improving with more data.*
  2. 模块化（层可以像积木一样堆叠）+ 对「层级结构」的归纳偏置（现实世界本来就是层级的：像素 → 边缘 → 物体），帮助对付维度灾难、泛化更好。

     *Modularity (layers stack like building blocks) plus an inductive bias towards hierarchy (the world is hierarchical: pixels → edges → objects), which helps against the curse of dimensionality and improves generalisation.*
  3. 可训练：有反向传播和 GPU。

     *Trainability: backpropagation and GPUs make it feasible.*
- 两个名词：**归纳偏置** = 模型结构里预先带着的假设，例如 CNN 假设「相邻像素关系更紧密」。**维度灾难** = 输入维度很高时（一张 224×224 的彩色图有约 15 万个数），要「覆盖」整个输入空间所需的数据量随维度指数增长。

  *Two terms. Inductive bias: an assumption built into the model's structure, e.g. a CNN assumes neighbouring pixels are closely related. Curse of dimensionality: with high-dimensional inputs (a 224×224 colour image has about 150,000 numbers), the data needed to cover the input space grows exponentially with the dimension.*

**4. 「神经」网络只是一个比喻 · "Neural" is only a metaphor**

课件原话的意思：神经网络和感知机只是**松散地**受生物启发，它们不是大脑工作方式的模型，也不是神经元工作方式的模型。判断题常考。

*From the slides: neural networks and perceptrons are only loosely inspired by biology. They are not a model of how the brain works, or even of how neurons work. A common true/false question.*

**5. 历史时间线 · Timeline**（知道先后顺序和代表人物即可 · know the order and the key people）

| 年份 · Year | 事件 · Event | 意义 · Why it matters |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1957 | 感知机 · Perceptron, Frank Rosenblatt | 第一个能学习的神经网络；Mark I 是硬件，20×20 光电管组成 400 像素「相机」，可调电阻当权重 · the first learning neural network; the Mark I was hardware with a 20×20 photocell "camera" and adjustable resistors as weights |
| 1974 | 反向传播 · Backpropagation, Paul Werbos | 训练多层网络的方法 · how to train multi-layer networks |
| 1980 | Neocognitron，福岛邦彦 · Kunihiko Fukushima | 受视觉皮层启发，CNN 的前身 · inspired by the visual cortex; precursor of CNNs |
| 1986 | RNN，David Rumelhart | 处理序列数据 · handles sequences |
| 1990 | LeNet，LeCun | 用 CNN 识别手写数字 · CNN for handwritten digits |
| 1998（课件标注 · as on the slide） | LSTM，Hochreiter & Schmidhuber | 解决 RNN 记不住长序列的问题 · fixes RNNs forgetting long sequences |
| 2006 | 深度信念网络 · Deep belief networks, Geoffrey Hinton | 深度学习复兴 · revival of deep learning |
| 2012 | AlexNet，Krizhevsky & Hinton | 用 GPU 训练，在 ImageNet 上大胜，引爆深度学习 · GPU-trained, won ImageNet by a wide margin, ignited deep learning |
| 2014 | GAN、GoogLeNet、VGG | 生成模型；更深的 CNN · generative models; deeper CNNs |
| 2015 | ResNet、AlphaGo | 残差连接让上百层可训练；围棋 · residual connections make 100+ layers trainable; Go |
| 2018–2020 | BERT、GPT-3 | 大规模预训练语言模型（L8、L9 详讲） · large pre-trained language models (L8, L9) |

**6. 常见网络家族 · Network families**：线性/多项式/逻辑回归；感知机和多层感知机 MLP（L4–L6）；卷积网络 CNN（图像）；循环网络 RNN（序列）；Transformer（注意力，L7–L10）；生成对抗网络 GAN；受限玻尔兹曼机、深度信念网络、脉冲神经网络、径向基函数网络、Hopfield 网络（知道名字即可）。

*Linear, polynomial and logistic regression; perceptrons and multi-layer perceptrons (L4–L6); convolutional networks (images); recurrent networks (sequences); Transformers (attention, L7–L10); GANs; restricted Boltzmann machines, deep belief networks, spiking networks, radial basis function networks and Hopfield networks (names only).*

**7. 应用 · Applications**：计算机视觉（图像分类、目标检测、场景文字识别、风格迁移、图像生成）；自然语言处理（问答、机器翻译）；语音（语音识别，如 Amazon Echo）。

*Computer vision (image classification, object detection, scene-text recognition, style transfer, image generation); NLP (question answering, machine translation); speech (speech recognition, e.g. Amazon Echo).*

**考试可能这样问 · Likely exam questions**

- 判断：深度学习是机器学习的子集。——对。

  *True/false: deep learning is a subset of machine learning. — True.*
- 判断：神经网络是大脑工作原理的模型。——错，只是松散地受启发。

  *True/false: neural networks model how the brain works. — False; they are only loosely inspired by it.*
- 简答：传统机器学习和深度学习的主要区别？——传统 ML 由人设计特征 φ(x)，只学分类器 w；深度学习用一个网络端到端地同时学特征和分类器。

  *Short answer: main difference between classical ML and DL? — Classical ML uses hand-designed features φ(x) and learns only the classifier w; deep learning learns features and classifier together, end to end, in one network.*
- 简答：为什么深度学习近年变得重要？——数据和算力增长；深度学习靠特征学习能吃到这份红利；模块化 + 层级归纳偏置帮助泛化；有反向传播和 GPU，可以训练。

  *Short answer: why has DL become important recently? — Data and compute have grown; feature learning lets DL exploit that growth; modularity and a hierarchical inductive bias help generalisation; backpropagation and GPUs make training feasible.*

### L2 机器学习基础与泛化误差 · ML Basics and Generalisation Error

**这一讲在干什么**：回答两个问题。① 为什么「训练集上表现好」不等于「真的好」？② 要多少数据，才能保证学到的东西在新数据上也好？第②问用 PAC 学习和 VC 维来回答。这是全课理论味最重的一讲，闭卷常考：定义、风险分解、有限类样本复杂度的推导与代数、VC 维的例子。

*What this lecture is about: two questions. (1) Why does doing well on the training set not mean doing well in reality? (2) How much data guarantees that what we learn also works on new data? The second is answered with PAC learning and the VC dimension. This is the most theoretical lecture; closed-book exams test the definitions, the risk decomposition, the derivation and algebra of the finite-class sample complexity, and VC-dimension examples.*

**0. 先补三个概率工具 · Three probability tools**

- **i.i.d.（独立同分布）**：每个样本都从同一个分布里独立抽出来，彼此互不影响。像反复掷同一枚硬币。

  *i.i.d. (independent and identically distributed): every sample is drawn independently from the same distribution, like repeatedly tossing the same coin.*
- **Hoeffding 不等式**（本讲的核心工具）：Z₁, …, Zₙ 独立、都在 \[0, 1\] 里、均值都是 μ，则样本平均偏离 μ 超过 ε 的概率至多 2e^(−2nε²)。直觉：硬币掷得越多，正面比例越接近真实概率，而且「偏离很多」的概率随 n 指数下降。

  *Hoeffding's inequality (the key tool): if Z₁, …, Zₙ are independent, lie in \[0, 1\] and have mean μ, the probability that their average deviates from μ by at least ε is at most 2e^(−2nε²). Intuition: the more tosses, the closer the fraction of heads to the true probability, and a large deviation becomes exponentially unlikely in n.*
  - 算例：n = 1000、ε = 0.05 → 2e^(−2·1000·0.0025) = 2e⁻⁵ ≈ 0.0135。即掷 1000 次，正面比例偏离真实值超过 5 个百分点的概率不到 1.4%。

    *Example: n = 1000, ε = 0.05 gives 2e⁻⁵ ≈ 0.0135, so after 1000 tosses the fraction of heads is more than 5 percentage points off with probability below 1.4%.*
- **并集界（union bound）**：P(A₁ 或 A₂ 或 … 或 Aₘ) ≤ P(A₁) + … + P(Aₘ)。不需要独立，永远成立。

  *Union bound: P(A₁ or … or Aₘ) ≤ P(A₁) + … + P(Aₘ). It needs no independence and always holds.*

```latex
\Pr\Bigl[\,\Bigl|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n} Z_i - \mu\Bigr| \ge \varepsilon\Bigr] \le 2e^{-2n\varepsilon^2}
```

**1. 动机 · Motivation**

- 训练算法几乎总能把训练误差压得很小，但我们真正关心的是没见过的数据。讲义的例子：把过去所有垃圾邮件都分对了，却把以后的大多分错，毫无用处。

  *Training algorithms nearly always reach small training error, but what matters is unseen data. The notes' example: classifying every past spam email correctly is useless if most future ones are wrong.*
- 模型太简单 → **欠拟合**（抓不住数据里的规律）；太复杂 → **过拟合**（把噪声和偶然的波动也当成规律，训练误差低、测试误差高）。正则化可以缓解过拟合（L6 详讲）。

  *Too simple a model underfits (misses the patterns); too complex a model overfits (treats noise and chance fluctuations as patterns: low training error, high test error). Regularisation reduces overfitting (see L6).*

**2. 课堂白板的两个点 · Two whiteboard points**

- **AI 中的偏差与伪相关**：某一组数据特别多时，模型容易学到「偶然同时出现」而不是「真正有关」的规律。常见举例（不是课件原例）：训练集里狼的照片大多带雪地背景，模型可能学成「有雪 = 狼」。白板给的对策：让各组数据量平衡。

  *Bias and spurious correlation in AI: when one group dominates the data, a model easily learns things that merely co-occur rather than things that are truly related. A common example (not from the slides): if most wolf photos have snowy backgrounds, the model may learn "snow = wolf". The whiteboard's remedy: balance the number of samples across groups.*
- **训练 vs 推理**：训练 = 前向传播 + 反向传播 + 更新参数；推理 = 只做前向传播。

  *Training vs inference: training = forward pass + backward pass + parameter update; inference = forward pass only.*

**3. 统计学习的基本设定（每个符号都要认得） · The statistical learning setup (know every symbol)**

- **数据集** D = {(xᵢ, yᵢ)}ᵢ₌₁ⁿ：每一对都 i.i.d. 地来自分布 P\_XY。**P\_XY 未知**——这是全部困难的来源。

  *Data set D = {(xᵢ, yᵢ)}: each pair is drawn i.i.d. from a distribution P\_XY, which is unknown — the source of all the difficulty.*
- **函数类（假设类）F**：我们允许挑选的所有函数。例如：所有线性分类器；所有不超过 p 次的多项式；1000 个决策桩的加权投票。

  *Function (hypothesis) class F: all the functions we may choose from, e.g. all linear classifiers, all polynomials up to degree p, or weighted votes of 1000 decision stumps.*
- **损失 ℓ(y, f(x))**：分类常用 0-1 损失 1{y ≠ f(x)}（错了记 1，对了记 0）；回归常用平方损失 (y − f(x))²；还有 hinge、logistic、指数损失。

  *Loss ℓ(y, f(x)): classification often uses the 0-1 loss 1{y ≠ f(x)} (1 if wrong, 0 if right); regression uses the squared loss (y − f(x))²; others include hinge, logistic and exponential losses.*
- **真实风险 R(f)**：在整个分布上的期望损失，也就是测试误差。如果知道分布，就能直接找到最优 f\* = argmin R(f)。

  *True risk R(f): the expected loss over the whole distribution, i.e. the test error. If the distribution were known we could find the best f directly.*
- **经验风险 Rₙ(f)**：训练集上的平均损失，也就是训练误差。因为分布未知，只好用它代替 R(f)。

  *Empirical risk Rₙ(f): the average loss on the training set, i.e. the training error. Since the distribution is unknown, we use it in place of R(f).*
- **经验风险最小化 ERM**：f\_erm = argmin\_{f∈F} Rₙ(f)，即「在函数类里挑训练误差最小的」。

  *Empirical risk minimisation (ERM): pick the function in F with the smallest training error.*
  - 线性模型 + logistic 损失的 ERM = 逻辑回归的最大似然；线性模型 + 平方损失的 ERM = 最小二乘（有闭式解）；一般地，损失取负对数似然时，ERM 就是最大似然。

    *ERM with a linear model and logistic loss is maximum-likelihood logistic regression; with squared loss it is least squares (closed form); in general, ERM with the negative log-likelihood as loss is maximum likelihood.*
  - 0-1 损失的 ERM 计算上不可行（不连续、不可导），实际会换成可导的替代损失（surrogate loss），比如交叉熵。

    *ERM with the 0-1 loss is computationally infeasible (discontinuous, not differentiable), so in practice a differentiable surrogate loss such as cross-entropy is used.*

```latex
R(f) = \mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim P_{XY}}\bigl[\ell(y, f(x))\bigr], \qquad R_n(f) = \frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ell\bigl(y_i, f(x_i)\bigr), \qquad f_{\mathrm{erm}} = \arg\min_{f\in\mathcal{F}} R_n(f)
```

**4. 风险分解：测试误差 = 训练误差 + 泛化误差 · Risk decomposition: test = train + generalisation error**

- 恒等式 R(f) = Rₙ(f) + \[R(f) − Rₙ(f)\]。方括号里的部分叫**泛化误差**（泛化间隙）。

  *The identity R(f) = Rₙ(f) + \[R(f) − Rₙ(f)\]; the bracket is the generalisation error (generalisation gap).*
- 训练误差小不代表测试误差小，因为泛化误差可能很大。核心问题就变成：**什么时候能保证泛化误差小？**

  *Small training error does not imply small test error, because the generalisation error can be large. So the central question is: when can we guarantee a small generalisation error?*
- 算例：训练错误率 2%、测试 15% → 泛化误差 13%，典型过拟合。训练 20%、测试 21% → 泛化误差只有 1%，但两者都高，是欠拟合。

  *Example: 2% training error and 15% test error give a 13% generalisation error — classic overfitting. 20% and 21% give only 1%, but both are high — underfitting.*

```latex
\underbrace{R(f)}_{\text{test error}} = \underbrace{R_n(f)}_{\text{training error}} + \underbrace{R(f) - R_n(f)}_{\text{generalization error}}
```

**5. PAC 学习（Probably Approximately Correct） · PAC learning**

- 大白话：只要样本够多，就能以很高的概率（**probably**）学到一个和 F 里最好的函数差不多（**approximately correct**）的函数。数据是随机的，不可能 100% 保证，所以只要求概率 ≥ 1 − δ。

  *In plain words: with enough samples we can, with high probability, learn a function whose risk is close to the best in F. Because the data are random, nothing is guaranteed with certainty, so we only ask for probability at least 1 − δ.*
- **正式定义**：函数类 F 是 PAC 可学的，如果存在算法 A 和函数 n(ε, δ)，使得对**任何**分布 P\_XY、任何 ε, δ ∈ (0, 1)，只要样本数 n ≥ n(ε, δ)，就有至少 1 − δ 的概率 R(f̂) ≤ min\_{f∈F} R(f) + ε。

  *Formal definition: F is PAC-learnable if there is an algorithm A and a function n(ε, δ) such that for every distribution P\_XY and every ε, δ in (0, 1), whenever n ≥ n(ε, δ), with probability at least 1 − δ we have R(f̂) ≤ min over F of R(f) + ε.*
  - 1 − δ：probably（δ 是失败概率，比如 0.05）。

    *1 − δ is the "probably" part (δ is the failure probability, e.g. 0.05).*
  - ε：approximately（允许和最好的差多少）。

    *ε is the "approximately" part (how far from the best we allow).*
  - n(ε, δ)：**样本复杂度**，达到要求至少要多少样本。

    *n(ε, δ) is the sample complexity: how many samples are needed.*
- **注意**：比较对象是 F 里最好的函数，不是「全世界最好的」。如果 F 只有线性分类器，而最好的线性分类器也有 10% 错误率，PAC 只保证你接近 10%。

  *Note: the benchmark is the best function in F, not the best function overall. If F holds only linear classifiers and the best of them has 10% error, PAC only promises you get close to 10%.*
- **可实现（realizable）**：F 里存在完美分类器，y = f(x)。**不可知（agnostic）**：F 里没有完美的，只和 F 里最好的比。两个原因会导致不可知：模型类不够强；或 y 本身对 x 就有随机性。下面的结果适用于 agnostic 情形。

  *Realizable: some f in F classifies perfectly (y = f(x)). Agnostic: no perfect f exists, so we only compete with the best in F — either because the class is too weak or because y is random given x. The results below are for the agnostic case.*
- 有些教材还要求算法的计算时间是多项式的；本课的定义不要求。

  *Some texts also require polynomial running time; this course's definition does not.*

```latex
\Pr\Bigl[\,R(\hat f) \le \min_{f\in\mathcal{F}} R(f) + \varepsilon\Bigr] \ge 1-\delta \quad \text{whenever } n \ge n(\varepsilon, \delta)
```

**6. 有限函数类定理（推导要会） · Finite function classes (know the proof)**

条件：损失取值在 \[0, 1\]，F 是有限集合。结论：F 是 PAC 可学的，样本复杂度 n(ε, δ) = (2/ε²)·log(2∣F∣/δ)。等价地，以至少 1 − δ 的概率，ERM 的风险比最好的函数多出不超过 √((2/n)·log(2∣F∣/δ))。

*Assumptions: the loss lies in \[0, 1\] and F is finite. Result: F is PAC-learnable with sample complexity n(ε, δ) = (2/ε²)·log(2∣F∣/δ). Equivalently, with probability at least 1 − δ, the ERM risk exceeds the best in F by at most √((2/n)·log(2∣F∣/δ)).*

```latex
n(\varepsilon,\delta) = \frac{2}{\varepsilon^2}\log\frac{2|\mathcal{F}|}{\delta} \quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad R(f_{\mathrm{erm}}) - \min_{f\in\mathcal{F}} R(f) \le \sqrt{\frac{2}{n}\log\frac{2|\mathcal{F}|}{\delta}}
```

证明分四步：

*The proof in four steps:*

1. **固定一个 f，用 Hoeffding**：对固定的 f，每个样本的损失 ℓ(yᵢ, f(xᵢ)) 是 i.i.d. 的 \[0, 1\] 随机变量，均值就是 R(f)，平均值就是 Rₙ(f)。所以 P(∣R(f) − Rₙ(f)∣ ≥ ε₀) ≤ 2e^(−2nε₀²)。

   *Fix one f and apply Hoeffding: for fixed f the per-sample losses are i.i.d. in \[0, 1\] with mean R(f) and average Rₙ(f), so P(∣R(f) − Rₙ(f)∣ ≥ ε₀) ≤ 2e^(−2nε₀²).*
2. **问题：f\_erm 不是固定的**：它是看了数据之后才选出来的，本身是随机变量，不能直接代入第 1 步。解决办法：要求 F 里**所有**函数同时满足偏差小，这叫**一致收敛**。

   *The catch: f\_erm is not fixed. It is chosen after seeing the data, so it is itself random and cannot be plugged into step 1. The fix: require the deviation to be small for all f in F simultaneously — uniform convergence.*
3. **并集界**：P(存在某个 f 使偏差 > ε₀) ≤ ∣F∣ × 2e^(−2nε₀²)。令它等于 δ，解得 n = (1/(2ε₀²))·log(2∣F∣/δ)。

   *Union bound: P(some f deviates by more than ε₀) ≤ ∣F∣ × 2e^(−2nε₀²). Setting this equal to δ gives n = (1/(2ε₀²))·log(2∣F∣/δ).*
4. **在「所有偏差都 ≤ ε₀」这个好事件上**，记 f\* 为 F 里真实风险最小的，把差拆成三段：R(f\_erm) − R(f\*) = \[R(f\_erm) − Rₙ(f\_erm)\] + \[Rₙ(f\_erm) − Rₙ(f\*)\] + \[Rₙ(f\*) − R(f\*)\] ≤ ε₀ + 0 + ε₀ = 2ε₀。中间一段 ≤ 0，因为 f\_erm 是训练误差最小的。取 ε₀ = ε/2，代入第 3 步得 n = (2/ε²)·log(2∣F∣/δ)。证毕。

   *On the good event where every deviation is at most ε₀, let f∗ be the true-risk minimiser in F and split the gap into three terms; the first and last are at most ε₀ and the middle is at most 0 because f\_erm minimises training error, giving 2ε₀. Taking ε₀ = ε/2 in step 3 gives n = (2/ε²)·log(2∣F∣/δ).*

```latex
R(f_{\mathrm{erm}}) - R(f^*) = \underbrace{R(f_{\mathrm{erm}}) - R_n(f_{\mathrm{erm}})}_{\le\,\varepsilon_0} + \underbrace{R_n(f_{\mathrm{erm}}) - R_n(f^*)}_{\le\,0} + \underbrace{R_n(f^*) - R(f^*)}_{\le\,\varepsilon_0} \le 2\varepsilon_0
```

- **算例**：100 位研究员各给一个分类器，∣F∣ = 100；要求 ε = 0.1、δ = 0.05。n = (2/0.01)·ln(200/0.05) = 200 × ln 4000 ≈ 200 × 8.29 ≈ 1659 个样本。

  *Example: 100 researchers each submit a classifier, so ∣F∣ = 100; with ε = 0.1 and δ = 0.05, n = 200 × ln 4000 ≈ 1659 samples.*
- 从公式看出的规律：∣F∣ 只在 log 里，函数类大 10 倍只多一点样本；ε 减半，n 变成 4 倍；大致要 n ≫ log∣F∣。

  *Patterns: ∣F∣ appears only inside the log, so a 10× larger class needs only a few more samples; halving ε quadruples n; roughly we need n ≫ log∣F∣.*
- 讲义的补充：可实现情形有更好的 1/ε 依赖（不是 1/ε²）；这是对最坏分布的界，对「容易」的分布可能过于悲观；其实任何只输出 F 内函数的算法都有小的泛化误差，不只是 ERM。

  *Notes from the lecture: the realizable case has a better 1/ε dependence (not 1/ε²); this is a worst-case bound and can be pessimistic for easy distributions; and the small generalisation error holds for any algorithm that outputs a function in F, not only ERM.*

**7. 无限函数类与 VC 维 · Infinite classes and the VC dimension**

- 线性分类器有无穷多个，∣F∣ = ∞，上面的公式失效。关键直觉：**在给定的 n 个点上，很多分类器给出的标注一模一样，「有效不同」的只有有限个**。

  *There are infinitely many linear classifiers, so ∣F∣ = ∞ and the formula breaks. Key intuition: on a given set of n points, many classifiers produce identical labellings, so only finitely many are effectively different.*
- 例 1：一维阈值分类器，x ≤ a 时输出 +1，否则 −1。把 n 个点排好序，a 落在相邻两点之间的任何位置效果都一样，所以最多 n + 1 种不同的标注。

  *Example 1: 1-D thresholds, +1 if x ≤ a and −1 otherwise. After sorting the n points, any a between the same two neighbours gives the same labels, so there are at most n + 1 labellings.*
- 例 2：区间分类器，x ∈ \[a, b\] 时输出 +1。a 和 b 各有 n + 1 种有效位置，最多 (n + 1)² 种。

  *Example 2: intervals, +1 if x ∈ \[a, b\]. Both a and b have n + 1 effective positions, so at most (n + 1)² labellings.*
- 推广：用 (n + 1)^d\_VC 代替 ∣F∣，取对数就是 d\_VC·log(n + 1) 代替 log∣F∣。这里的 d\_VC 就是 VC 维。

  *Generalisation: replace ∣F∣ by (n + 1)^d\_VC, i.e. log∣F∣ by d\_VC·log(n + 1), where d\_VC is the VC dimension.*

三个定义：

*Three definitions:*

- **增长函数 Sₙ(F)**（又叫打散数）：在最有利的 n 个点上，F 能产生的不同标注向量 (f(x₁), …, f(xₙ)) 的个数，介于 1 和 2ⁿ 之间。

  *Growth function (shattering number) Sₙ(F): the largest number of distinct label vectors F can produce on any n points; between 1 and 2ⁿ.*
- **打散（shatter）**：F 能在某 k 个点上实现全部 2ᵏ 种标注。

  *Shatter: F realises all 2ᵏ labellings of some k points.*
- **VC 维 d\_VC**：能被 F 打散的最大点数 k；如果多少点都能打散，则 d\_VC = ∞。

  *VC dimension: the largest k such that some k points are shattered; ∞ if every size can be shattered.*
- **证明 d\_VC = k 必须两步**：① **存在**某 k 个点能被打散（挑一组好摆的点就行）；② **任意** k + 1 个点都不能被打散（要对所有摆法成立）。这是考试最常见的失分点。

  *Proving d\_VC = k takes two steps: (1) some set of k points can be shattered (one good arrangement suffices); (2) no set of k + 1 points can be shattered (must hold for every arrangement). Missing step 2 is the most common lost mark.*
- **Sauer 引理**：Sₙ(F) ≤ Σ\_{i=0}^{d\_VC} C(n, i) ≤ (n + 1)^d\_VC；n > d\_VC 时还有 Sₙ(F) ≤ (en/d\_VC)^d\_VC。

  *Sauer's lemma bounds the growth function by a polynomial in n of degree d\_VC.*
- **定理**：d\_VC 有限 ⇒ F 在 0-1 损失下 PAC 可学，样本复杂度 n = C·(d\_VC + log(1/δ))/ε²；反过来，d\_VC = ∞ 就不是 PAC 可学的。直觉：如果 d\_VC 个点能被打散，而你只观察到其中一半，另一半的标签完全无从推断，所以样本数至少要和 d\_VC 同量级。

  *Theorem: finite d\_VC implies PAC-learnability under the 0-1 loss with n = C·(d\_VC + log(1/δ))/ε²; infinite d\_VC means not PAC-learnable. Intuition: if d\_VC points can be shattered and you see only half of them, the other half's labels are completely undetermined, so you need on the order of d\_VC samples.*

```latex
S_n(\mathcal{F}) \le \sum_{i=0}^{d_{VC}}\binom{n}{i} \le (n+1)^{d_{VC}}, \qquad n(\varepsilon,\delta) = C\cdot\frac{d_{VC} + \log(1/\delta)}{\varepsilon^2}
```

**VC 维例子（必背，连理由一起背） · VC-dimension examples (memorise with the reasons)**

| 函数类 · Class | VC 维 · d\_VC | 为什么 k 个点能打散 · Why k points can be shattered | 为什么 k + 1 个点不能 · Why k + 1 cannot |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 一维阈值（x ≤ a 为 +1） · 1-D threshold | 1 | 1 个点：a 放在它右边得 +，放在左边得 − · one point: a to its right gives +, to its left gives − | 2 个点 x₁ < x₂：标注 (−, +) 做不到 · two points x₁ < x₂: the labelling (−, +) is impossible because + can only be on the left |
| 一维区间（\[a, b\] 内为 +1） · 1-D interval | 2 | 2 个点的 4 种标注都能做出 · all 4 labellings of 2 points work | x₁ < x₂ < x₃ 标 (+, −, +) 做不到 · (+, −, +) is impossible: an interval containing x₁ and x₃ contains x₂ |
| 二维轴对齐矩形 · 2-D axis-aligned rectangle | 4 | 摆成菱形的 4 个点 · 4 points in a diamond; all 16 labellings can be boxed | 5 个点：最左、最右、最上、最下标 +，第 5 个无法是 − · 5 points: label the leftmost, rightmost, top and bottom +; the rectangle around them also contains the fifth, which cannot be − |
| 二维线性分类器（带偏置） · 2-D linear classifier with bias | 3 | 不共线的 3 点，8 种标注都能分开 · 3 non-collinear points; all 8 labellings are separable | 凸四边形的对角同号做不到；若一点在三角形内也做不到 · convex quadrilateral: opposite corners labelled alike fail because the diagonals cross; if one point lies inside the triangle of the others, inside + and corners − fails |
| d 维线性分类器 · d-D linear classifier | 带偏置 d + 1，不带 d · d + 1 with bias, d without | — | — |
| 有限类 F · finite class | ≤ log₂∣F∣ | 打散 k 个点要 ∣F∣ ≥ 2ᵏ · shattering k points needs ∣F∣ ≥ 2ᵏ | — |
| 所有函数 · all functions | ∞ | 任意点都能打散 · any distinct points | 不是 PAC 可学的 · not PAC-learnable |

- 「线性分类器的 VC 维 = 维数（+1）」只是巧合，非线性类不一定成立（二维矩形是 4）。

  *"VC dimension = dimension (+1)" holds for linear classifiers but not in general (2-D rectangles have 4).*
- 线性分类器「交叉对角」论证用到的事实：如果线性分类器把两个点分到同一类，那么它们连线上的所有点也在同一类。

  *The fact behind the crossed-diagonals argument: if a linear classifier puts two points in the same class, every point on the segment between them is in that class too.*

**8. 选读：其他泛化工具 · Optional: other generalisation tools**

- **Rademacher 复杂度**：给每个样本随机分配 ±1 的标签，看 F 平均能拟合这些随机标签到什么程度。越能拟合纯噪声，越容易过拟合。它给出泛化界：以概率 ≥ 1 − δ，所有 f 的 R(f) − Rₙ(f) ≤ ℛₙ(F) + √(log(2/δ)/(2n))。

  *Rademacher complexity: assign random ±1 labels and measure how well F can fit them on average. The better it fits pure noise, the easier it overfits. It gives the bound R(f) − Rₙ(f) ≤ ℛₙ(F) + √(log(2/δ)/(2n)) for all f, with probability at least 1 − δ.*
- 其他能保证泛化的概念：只有少数数据点「起作用」（如 SVM 的支持向量）、大间隔（margin）、算法稳定性、鲁棒性、PAC-Bayes。

  *Other routes to generalisation: few relevant points (SVM support vectors), large margin, algorithmic stability, robustness, PAC-Bayes.*

**本讲易错点 · Common mistakes**

- PAC 比较的是 F 中最好的函数，不是全局最优。

  *PAC compares with the best function in F, not the global optimum.*
- VC 维定义里是「存在」k 个点能被打散，而不是「任意」k 个点；但证明上界时要说明「任意」k + 1 个点都不行。

  *The VC definition needs some k points to be shattered, not every k points; but the upper bound must show that every set of k + 1 points fails.*
- 泛化误差指的是 R − Rₙ 这个差，不是测试误差本身。

  *Generalisation error means the gap R − Rₙ, not the test error itself.*
- 不可知情形样本复杂度是 1/ε²，可实现情形是 1/ε。

  *Sample complexity scales as 1/ε² in the agnostic case and 1/ε in the realizable case.*
- 有限类证明里不能对 f\_erm 直接用 Hoeffding，要先用并集界做一致收敛。

  *In the finite-class proof you cannot apply Hoeffding to f\_erm directly; you need the union bound for uniform convergence first.*

### L3 训练算法基础：梯度下降 · Training Algorithm Basics: Gradient Descent

**这一讲在干什么**：L2 说要最小化训练损失，这一讲讲「具体怎么最小化」：梯度下降家族。闭卷重点：为什么沿负梯度走（会证明）；GD、SGD、mini-batch 的区别和优缺点；学习率收敛/发散分析（quiz 原题）；逻辑回归的梯度；动量。

*What this lecture is about: L2 says we minimise the training loss; this lecture is about how — the gradient descent family. Exam focus: why we move along the negative gradient (with proof); the differences, pros and cons of GD, SGD and mini-batch; learning-rate convergence/divergence analysis (the quiz question); the gradient of logistic regression; momentum.*

**1. 优化问题长什么样 · The optimisation problem**

- 深度学习几乎都在解：min\_θ (1/n)Σ Loss(xₜ, yₜ; θ)，可能再加一个正则项 λ·Reg(θ)（如 λ‖θ‖²）。θ 是所有参数（神经网络的全部权重）。

  *Nearly all of deep learning solves min over θ of (1/n)Σ Loss(xₜ, yₜ; θ), possibly plus a regulariser λ·Reg(θ) such as λ‖θ‖². θ is every parameter (all the network's weights).*
- 讲义为了通用，把它写成 min\_x f(x)，f(x) = (1/n)Σ fᵢ(x)。**注意这里的 x 是参数（相当于 θ），不是数据**；fᵢ 是第 i 个样本的损失。闭卷时别被符号搞混。

  *The lecture notes write it generically as min over x of f(x) = (1/n)Σ fᵢ(x). Here x is the parameter vector (playing the role of θ), not the data; fᵢ is the loss on sample i. Don't mix up the symbols.*

```latex
\min_{x\in\mathbb{R}^d} f(x), \qquad f(x) = \frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n} f_i(x)
```

**2. 梯度是什么 · What a gradient is**

- 梯度 ∇f = (∂f/∂x₁, …, ∂f/∂x\_d)ᵀ，第 j 个分量是「只动第 j 个参数时 f 的变化率」。它指向 f 上升最快的方向。

  *The gradient ∇f = (∂f/∂x₁, …, ∂f/∂x\_d)ᵀ; component j is the rate of change of f when only parameter j moves. It points in the direction of fastest increase.*
- 例：f(x₁, x₂) = x₁² + 3x₂²，则 ∇f = (2x₁, 6x₂)；在 (1, 1) 处 ∇f = (2, 6)。

  *Example: f(x₁, x₂) = x₁² + 3x₂² has ∇f = (2x₁, 6x₂), which is (2, 6) at (1, 1).*

**3. 梯度下降 GD · Gradient descent**

- 想象成在山地里下山：从某个起点出发，反复朝「最陡的下坡方向」走一小步，直到走不动。

  *Picture walking down a landscape: from a starting point, repeatedly take a small step in the steepest downhill direction until you cannot go lower.*
- 更新公式：x\_next = x − η∇f(x)，η > 0 是步长（学习率）。

  *Update rule: x\_next = x − η∇f(x), where η > 0 is the step size (learning rate).*
- **算例**（接上面的 f）：η = 0.1，从 (1, 1) 出发。x\_next = (1 − 0.1·2, 1 − 0.1·6) = (0.8, 0.4)。f 从 1 + 3 = 4 降到 0.64 + 0.48 = 1.12。

  *Worked example (same f): with η = 0.1 from (1, 1), x\_next = (0.8, 0.4) and f drops from 4 to 0.64 + 0.48 = 1.12.*

```latex
x_{\text{next}} = x - \eta\,\nabla f(x), \qquad \nabla f = \Bigl(\frac{\partial f}{\partial x_1}, \dots, \frac{\partial f}{\partial x_d}\Bigr)^{\!\top}
```

**为什么负梯度是下降最快的方向（会证） · Why the negative gradient is the steepest descent direction (proof)**

1. 从 x 沿单位方向 Δ（‖Δ‖ = 1）走很小的一步 ε。一阶 Taylor 展开：f(x + εΔ) = f(x) + ε⟨∇f(x), Δ⟩ + O(ε²)。

   *Take a tiny step ε from x along a unit direction Δ (‖Δ‖ = 1). First-order Taylor: f(x + εΔ) = f(x) + ε⟨∇f(x), Δ⟩ + O(ε²).*
2. ε 很小，O(ε²) 可以忽略。要让 f 下降最多，就要 ⟨∇f, Δ⟩ 尽可能负。

   *For small ε the O(ε²) term is negligible, so to decrease f the most we want ⟨∇f, Δ⟩ as negative as possible.*
3. Cauchy–Schwarz：∣⟨∇f, Δ⟩∣ ≤ ‖∇f‖·‖Δ‖ = ‖∇f‖，等号当且仅当 Δ 与 ∇f 平行。

   *Cauchy–Schwarz: ∣⟨∇f, Δ⟩∣ ≤ ‖∇f‖·‖Δ‖ = ‖∇f‖, with equality exactly when Δ is parallel to ∇f.*
4. 所以内积的最小值是 −‖∇f‖，在 Δ = −∇f/‖∇f‖ 时取到，即负梯度方向。

   *So the smallest possible inner product is −‖∇f‖, reached at Δ = −∇f/‖∇f‖: the negative gradient direction.*

```latex
f(x + \varepsilon\Delta) = f(x) + \varepsilon\,\langle \nabla f(x), \Delta\rangle + O(\varepsilon^2), \qquad \langle \nabla f, \Delta\rangle \ge -\|\nabla f\|\,\|\Delta\|
```

**局部最小 vs 全局最小**：GD 可能卡在一个不是最低的坑（局部最小值）。但如果 f 是**凸函数**（碗形，任两点连线在函数图像上方），任何局部最小都是全局最小。线性回归的平方损失、逻辑回归的交叉熵是凸的；多层神经网络的损失不是凸的。

*Local vs global minimum: GD can get stuck in a pit that is not the lowest one (a local minimum). If f is convex (bowl-shaped: the chord between any two points lies above the graph), every local minimum is global. The squared loss of linear regression and the cross-entropy of logistic regression are convex; the loss of a multi-layer network is not.*

**4. 随机梯度下降 SGD 与 mini-batch · Stochastic gradient descent and mini-batches**

- GD 每一步要对 n 个样本都求梯度再平均（和的梯度 = 梯度的和）。n 很大时，每步都要算 n 项，很贵，而一步未必前进多少。

  *Each GD step averages the gradients of all n samples (the gradient of a sum is the sum of gradients). For large n every step costs n gradient evaluations, which is expensive and may not buy much progress.*
- **SGD**：每步只用一个样本 i 的梯度，x\_next = x − η∇fᵢ(x)。i 可以按顺序轮流取，也可以均匀随机抽。

  *SGD: use the gradient of a single sample i per step, x\_next = x − η∇fᵢ(x); i is taken in turn or uniformly at random.*
  - 「平均而言」方向是对的：i 均匀随机时 E\[∇fᵢ(x)\] = ∇f(x)，是无偏估计。

    *On average the direction is right: for uniform random i, E\[∇fᵢ(x)\] = ∇f(x), an unbiased estimate.*
  - 路径会来回抖动。这不全是坏事：抖动能帮助跳出局部最小。

    *The path jitters, which is not all bad: the noise can help escape local minima.*
- **Mini-batch SGD**：每步取一小批样本 I（讲义举 5、10、20，深度学习常用 256 等），用它们的平均梯度。∣I∣ = n 就是 GD，∣I∣ = 1 就是 SGD。

  *Mini-batch SGD: average the gradient over a small batch I (5, 10 or 20 in the notes; 256 and similar in deep learning). ∣I∣ = n is GD and ∣I∣ = 1 is SGD.*

```latex
\text{GD: } x \leftarrow x - \eta\,\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\nabla f_i(x), \qquad \text{SGD: } x \leftarrow x - \eta\,\nabla f_i(x), \qquad \text{mini-batch: } x \leftarrow x - \eta\,\frac{1}{|I|}\sum_{i\in I}\nabla f_i(x)
```

| 方法 · Method | 每步用多少样本 · Samples per step | 每步代价 · Cost per step | 方向 · Direction | 特点 · Behaviour |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| GD | 全部 n 个 · all n | 最贵 · highest | 准确 · exact | 平稳；卡住就一直卡住 · smooth; once stuck, stays stuck |
| SGD | 1 个 · one | 最便宜 · lowest | 噪声大、平均正确 · noisy but unbiased | 路径抖，能跳出坑 · jittery, can escape pits |
| Mini-batch | b 个 · b samples | 居中 · moderate | 较稳 · fairly stable | 实际最常用，能充分利用 GPU · the usual choice; uses the GPU well |

**5. 例：逻辑回归的 GD 和 SGD · Example: GD and SGD for logistic regression**

- 这里的标签 y ∈ {−1, +1}（不是 0/1）。损失 log(1 + exp(−y(θᵀx + θ₀)))。

  *Here labels are y ∈ {−1, +1} (not 0/1) and the loss is log(1 + exp(−y(θᵀx + θ₀))).*
- 记 P(y ∣ x) = 1/(1 + exp(−y(θᵀx + θ₀)))，就是模型给「正确标签」的概率。

  *Write P(y ∣ x) = 1/(1 + exp(−y(θᵀx + θ₀))): the probability the model gives to the correct label.*
- 梯度：∂/∂θ₀ = −y(1 − P(y ∣ x))，∂/∂θ = −y·x·(1 − P(y ∣ x))。

  *Gradients: ∂/∂θ₀ = −y(1 − P(y ∣ x)) and ∂/∂θ = −y·x·(1 − P(y ∣ x)).*
- 推导：令 u = y(θᵀx + θ₀)，损失 = log(1 + e⁻ᵘ)，对 u 求导得 −e⁻ᵘ/(1 + e⁻ᵘ) = −(1 − σ(u))，而 σ(u) 正好是 P(y ∣ x)；再乘以 ∂u/∂θ = y·x。

  *Derivation: let u = y(θᵀx + θ₀), so the loss is log(1 + e⁻ᵘ) with derivative −e⁻ᵘ/(1 + e⁻ᵘ) = −(1 − σ(u)), and σ(u) is exactly P(y ∣ x); multiply by ∂u/∂θ = y·x.*
- 更新（注意减去负号变成加号）：GD 是 θ ← θ + η·(1/n)Σ yₜxₜ(1 − P(yₜ ∣ xₜ))；SGD 去掉求和、只用一个 t。

  *Updates (subtracting a negative gradient becomes a plus): GD uses θ ← θ + η·(1/n)Σ yₜxₜ(1 − P(yₜ ∣ xₜ)); SGD drops the sum and uses one t.*
- 直觉：模型已经很自信地答对时 P ≈ 1，更新几乎为 0；错得越离谱，更新越大。

  *Intuition: when the model is already confidently right, P ≈ 1 and the update is almost zero; the more wrong it is, the bigger the update.*
- **算例**：x = (1, 2)、y = +1、θ = (0, 0)、θ₀ = 0、η = 0.1。P = σ(0) = 0.5；对 θ 的梯度 = −1·(1, 2)·0.5 = (−0.5, −1)，对 θ₀ 的梯度 = −0.5。更新后 θ = (0.05, 0.1)、θ₀ = 0.05。

  *Worked example: x = (1, 2), y = +1, θ = (0, 0), θ₀ = 0, η = 0.1. Then P = σ(0) = 0.5, the gradient for θ is (−0.5, −1) and for θ₀ is −0.5, so after the update θ = (0.05, 0.1) and θ₀ = 0.05.*
- 讲义的图：GD 和 SGD 都朝同一个方向走，SGD 走得弯弯曲曲；用 mini-batch 或逐渐减小步长可以让它平稳一些。

  *The notes' figure: GD and SGD head the same way, SGD much more erratically; mini-batches or a decaying step size make it smoother.*

```latex
\frac{\partial}{\partial \theta}\log\bigl(1+e^{-y(\theta^\top x+\theta_0)}\bigr) = -y\,x\,\bigl(1 - P(y\mid x;\theta,\theta_0)\bigr)
```

**6. 学习率对收敛的影响（Quiz 原题，必会） · Learning rate and convergence (quiz question, must know)**

题目：f(θ) = ½θ²，步长 α ≥ 0。

*Problem: f(θ) = ½θ² with step size α ≥ 0.*

- (a) 导数 f′(θ) = θ，更新 θ⁽ᵏ⁺¹⁾ = θ⁽ᵏ⁾ − αθ⁽ᵏ⁾ = (1 − α)θ⁽ᵏ⁾。

  *(a) f′(θ) = θ, so the update is θ⁽ᵏ⁺¹⁾ = (1 − α)θ⁽ᵏ⁾.*
- (b) α = 1 时 θ⁽¹⁾ = 0，一步到达最优点，**与初始值无关**。

  *(b) With α = 1, θ⁽¹⁾ = 0: the optimum in one step, whatever the starting point.*
- (c) 反复代入得 θ⁽ᵏ⁾ = (1 − α)ᵏ θ⁽⁰⁾。利用「∣q∣ < 1 时 qᵏ → 0，∣q∣ > 1 时 → ∞」：

  *(c) Unrolling gives θ⁽ᵏ⁾ = (1 − α)ᵏθ⁽⁰⁾. Since qᵏ → 0 when ∣q∣ < 1 and → ∞ when ∣q∣ > 1:*
  - 0 < α < 2：∣1 − α∣ < 1，收敛到 0。

    *0 < α < 2: ∣1 − α∣ < 1, converges to 0.*
  - α = 2：1 − α = −1，在 θ⁽⁰⁾ 和 −θ⁽⁰⁾ 之间永远来回跳（设 θ⁽⁰⁾ ≠ 0）。

    *α = 2: 1 − α = −1, oscillates between θ⁽⁰⁾ and −θ⁽⁰⁾ forever (if θ⁽⁰⁾ ≠ 0).*
  - α > 2：∣1 − α∣ > 1，发散到无穷大。

    *α > 2: ∣1 − α∣ > 1, diverges to infinity.*

```latex
\theta^{(k)} = (1-\alpha)^k\,\theta^{(0)} \;\Rightarrow\; \begin{cases} 0<\alpha<2: & \theta^{(k)}\to 0 \\ \alpha = 2: & \pm\theta^{(0)} \text{ forever} \\ \alpha > 2: & |\theta^{(k)}|\to\infty \end{cases}
```

数值感受一下（θ⁽⁰⁾ = 1）：

*Numbers to get a feel (θ⁽⁰⁾ = 1):*

| α | 前几步的 θ · First iterates | 行为 · Behaviour |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 0.5 | 1, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125 | 单调收敛 · monotone convergence |
| 1 | 1, 0, 0, 0 | 一步到位 · one step |
| 1.5 | 1, −0.5, 0.25, −0.125 | 来回跳但收敛 · oscillating convergence |
| 2 | 1, −1, 1, −1 | 永远振荡 · oscillates forever |
| 3 | 1, −2, 4, −8 | 发散 · diverges |

- 推广（帮助理解）：对 f = (c/2)θ²，更新因子是 1 − αc，收敛条件是 0 < α < 2/c。曲线越陡（c 越大），学习率就必须越小。

  *Generalisation (for intuition): for f = (c/2)θ² the factor is 1 − αc, so convergence needs 0 < α < 2/c. The steeper the curve (larger c), the smaller the learning rate must be.*
- 启示：学习率太大会振荡甚至发散，太小则收敛很慢。

  *Lesson: too large a learning rate oscillates or diverges; too small converges slowly.*

**7. 投影梯度法（有约束时） · Projected gradient (with constraints)**

- 问题变成 min f(x) 且 x ∈ C。做法：每步先普通更新，再「投影」回 C：x\_next = Π\_C(x − ηg)。Π\_C(x′) 是 C 里离 x′ 最近的点。

  *The problem becomes min f(x) subject to x ∈ C. Take an ordinary step, then project back onto C: x\_next = Π\_C(x − ηg), where Π\_C(x′) is the point of C closest to x′.*
- 例：约束是每个坐标 aᵢ ≤ xᵢ ≤ bᵢ，投影就是「超出就截断」。约束 \[0, 1\]，更新后得 1.3 就变 1，得 −0.2 就变 0。

  *Example: box constraints aᵢ ≤ xᵢ ≤ bᵢ, where projection simply clips. With \[0, 1\], an update to 1.3 becomes 1 and −0.2 becomes 0.*
- 要求 C 是凸集；收敛分析可以推广到次梯度和随机次梯度。

  *C must be convex; the convergence analysis extends to subgradients and stochastic subgradients.*

**8. 动量（momentum） · Momentum**

- 问题：在狭长的山谷里，GD 会在两壁之间来回振荡，沿谷底前进很慢。

  *Problem: in a long narrow valley GD bounces between the walls and moves slowly along the floor.*
- 做法：记住上一步的一部分。讲义写法 vₖ = η∇f(xₖ) + γvₖ₋₁，xₖ₊₁ = xₖ − vₖ；L6 写法 v ← βv + g，w ← w − αv。两者本质相同，只是学习率乘在哪里不同。γ = 0 就退回普通 GD，常取 0.9。

  *Fix: remember part of the previous step. The notes write vₖ = η∇f(xₖ) + γvₖ₋₁, xₖ₊₁ = xₖ − vₖ; L6 writes v ← βv + g, w ← w − αv. They are the same idea; only where the learning rate multiplies differs. γ = 0 recovers plain GD; 0.9 is typical.*
- 直觉：来回振荡的方向上梯度正负交替，累加后互相抵消；一直同向的方向上不断累加、越走越快。

  *Intuition: in the oscillating direction the gradient alternates sign and the accumulated terms cancel; in a consistent direction they add up and the steps grow.*
- **算例**（v ← 0.9v + g，v 初始为 0）：

  *Worked example (v ← 0.9v + g, starting from v = 0):*
  - 梯度一直是 +1：v = 1, 1.9, 2.71, 3.44, …，最终趋于 1/(1 − 0.9) = 10，等于把步长放大了 10 倍。

    *Gradient always +1: v = 1, 1.9, 2.71, 3.44, … tending to 1/(1 − 0.9) = 10, a tenfold larger step.*
  - 梯度在 +1 和 −1 之间交替：v = 1, −0.1, 0.91, −0.18, …，幅度远小于 1，振荡被压下去了。

    *Gradient alternating +1, −1: v = 1, −0.1, 0.91, −0.18, …, much smaller than 1: the oscillation is damped.*

```latex
v_k = \eta\,\nabla f(x_k) + \gamma\, v_{k-1}, \qquad x_{k+1} = x_k - v_k
```

**9. 其他方法（知道名字和一句话） · Other methods (name and one line)**

- **线搜索**：每步试几个步长，挑函数值最好的；更稳但更费算力。

  *Line search: try several step sizes each iteration and keep the best; more robust but more compute.*
- **Adagrad / RMSprop / Adam**：自适应学习率，L6 详讲。

  *Adagrad / RMSprop / Adam: adaptive learning rates, covered in L6.*
- **分布式优化**：一台机器算不动时多机并行。

  *Distributed optimisation: many machines in parallel when one is not enough.*
- **二阶方法**（牛顿法）：用二阶导数。d 个参数就有 d² 个二阶导数（而梯度只有 d 个），大模型存不下、算不起，只能用便宜的近似。

  *Second-order methods (Newton): use second derivatives. d parameters have d² second derivatives (versus d gradient entries), too many for large models, so only cheap approximations are used.*
- **零阶方法**（黑盒优化）：拿不到梯度，只能查询函数值 f(x)。

  *Zeroth-order (black-box) methods: no gradients available, only queries of f(x).*
- **MeZO**（本讲阅读论文，只用前向传播微调大语言模型）：用两次前向传播估计梯度——沿一个随机方向 z 把参数各加、减一点点，用损失之差除以 2ε 再乘 z。不需要反向传播，就不用存激活和梯度，所以微调的显存和推理一样：一张 A100 80GB 用 MeZO 能微调 30B 模型，用反向传播只能微调 2.7B。还能优化不可导的目标（如准确率）。

  *MeZO (this lecture's reading: fine-tuning LLMs with forward passes only): estimate the gradient from two forward passes — nudge the parameters up and down along a random direction z and multiply the loss difference over 2ε by z. No backpropagation means no stored activations or gradients, so fine-tuning needs only inference memory: one 80 GB A100 can fine-tune a 30B model with MeZO but only 2.7B with backprop. It can also optimise non-differentiable objectives such as accuracy.*

```latex
\hat g = \frac{f(\theta + \varepsilon z) - f(\theta - \varepsilon z)}{2\varepsilon}\, z, \qquad z \sim \mathcal{N}(0, I)
```

**本讲易错点 · Common mistakes**

- 更新是**减**梯度；逻辑回归里梯度本身带负号，减后变加号。

  *The update subtracts the gradient; in logistic regression the gradient carries a minus sign, so the update becomes a plus.*
- 学习率题一定要写出 θ⁽ᵏ⁾ = (1 − α)ᵏθ⁽⁰⁾，再分 ∣q∣ < 1、= 1、> 1 三种情况。α = 2 是振荡不是收敛。

  *In the learning-rate question, write θ⁽ᵏ⁾ = (1 − α)ᵏθ⁽⁰⁾ and split into ∣q∣ < 1, = 1, > 1. α = 2 oscillates; it does not converge.*
- SGD 的抖动不全是缺点，考简答时要两面都说。

  *SGD's noise is not only a drawback; give both sides in a short answer.*
- 凸函数才有「局部最小 = 全局最小」，神经网络不是凸的。

  *"Local minimum = global minimum" holds only for convex functions; neural networks are not convex.*

### L4 线性回归 · Linear Regression

**这一讲在干什么**：用最简单的模型把完整流程走一遍：数据 → 模型 → 损失 → 求导 → 梯度下降。后面所有神经网络都是这个流程的放大版。闭卷常考：手算梯度和一步更新；向量化梯度公式和形状检查；求导法则；解析解。

*What this lecture is about: run the whole pipeline once with the simplest model — data → model → loss → derivative → gradient descent. Every later network is a scaled-up version of this. Exam favourites: computing a gradient and one update by hand; the vectorised gradient and shape checks; derivative rules; the closed-form solution.*

**1. 问题和符号 · Problem and notation**

- 例子：用身高（英寸）预测体重（磅），数据来自 Kaggle；80% 用来训练，20% 用来评估。

  *Example: predict weight (pounds) from height (inches) using Kaggle data; 80% for training, 20% for evaluation.*
- 一个人 = 一个**样本**（example / instance）；身高 x ∈ ℝ 是**输入特征**；体重 y ∈ ℝ 是**目标**（ground truth，真值）。

  *One person = one example (instance); height x ∈ ℝ is the input feature; weight y ∈ ℝ is the target (ground truth).*
- 第 i 个样本记作 (x⁽ⁱ⁾, y⁽ⁱ⁾)。**上标带括号表示第几个样本，不是乘方**；下标 xᵢ 表示向量的第 i 个分量。

  *The i-th example is (x⁽ⁱ⁾, y⁽ⁱ⁾). A bracketed superscript indexes the example, not a power; a subscript xᵢ is the i-th component of a vector.*
- **模型**：ỹ = xw + b。w 是斜率（身高每多 1 英寸，体重多几磅），b 是截距，ỹ 是预测值。w、b 就是要学的参数。

  *Model: ỹ = xw + b. w is the slope (extra pounds per extra inch), b the intercept, ỹ the prediction; w and b are the parameters to learn.*

**2. 损失函数和训练目标 · Loss and training objective**

- 损失衡量「一个样本上预测差多少」，越小越好。

  *The loss measures how far off one prediction is; smaller is better.*
  - 绝对值损失 ∣ỹ − y∣：在误差为 0 处不可导。

    *Absolute loss ∣ỹ − y∣: not differentiable at zero error.*
  - **平方损失** ½(ỹ − y)²：处处可导，更好优化；前面的 ½ 只是为了求导后和指数 2 抵消，不影响最优解。

    *Squared loss ½(ỹ − y)²: differentiable everywhere and easier to optimise; the ½ just cancels the 2 from differentiation and does not change the optimum.*
- **训练目标** J(w, b) = (1/m)Σ L⁽ⁱ⁾：所有 m 个训练样本的平均损失。训练就是求 min\_{w,b} J(w, b)。

  *Training objective J(w, b) = (1/m)Σ L⁽ⁱ⁾, the average loss over all m training examples. Training means minimising J over w and b.*
- 固定 b 时，J 是 w 的二次函数 c₁w² + c₂w + c₃（c₁ > 0），是一个开口向上的碗，只有一个最低点；固定 w 时对 b 也一样。

  *With b fixed, J is a quadratic c₁w² + c₂w + c₃ in w (c₁ > 0): an upward bowl with a single minimum. The same holds for b with w fixed.*

```latex
\tilde y = xw + b, \qquad L = \tfrac12(\tilde y - y)^2, \qquad J(w,b) = \frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m} \tfrac12\bigl(x^{(i)}w + b - y^{(i)}\bigr)^2
```

**3. 求导法则（闭卷必须熟练） · Derivative rules (must be fluent)**

- 定义：f′(x) = lim\_{h→0} \[f(x + h) − f(x)\]/h，就是曲线在该点的斜率。

  *Definition: f′(x) = lim as h → 0 of \[f(x + h) − f(x)\]/h, the slope of the curve at that point.*

| 法则 · Rule | 公式 · Formula | 例子 · Example |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 常数 · constant | (c)′ = 0 | (3)′ = 0 |
| 线性 · linear | (cx)′ = c | (3x)′ = 3 |
| 幂 · power | (xⁿ)′ = nxⁿ⁻¹ | (x²)′ = 2x |
| 和 · sum | (g + h)′ = g′ + h′ | (3x + x²)′ = 3 + 2x |
| 积 · product | (gh)′ = g′h + gh′ | (x·x²)′ = x² + x·2x = 3x² |
| 商 · quotient | (g/h)′ = (g′h − gh′)/h² | (eˣ/x)′ = (xeˣ − eˣ)/x² |
| 链式 · chain | \[g(h(x))\]′ = g′(h(x))·h′(x) | \[ln(x + x²)\]′ = (1 + 2x)/(x + x²) |
| 指数、对数 · exp, log | (eˣ)′ = eˣ；(ln x)′ = 1/x | (e⁻ˣ)′ = −e⁻ˣ |

- **次梯度**（不可导点怎么办）：对 ∣x∣，x ≠ 0 时导数是 x 的符号（±1）；x = 0 处不可导，取 \[−1, 1\] 里任意一个值都算次梯度。ReLU 在 0 点也用这个办法。

  *Subgradient (at non-differentiable points): for ∣x∣ the derivative is the sign of x (±1) when x ≠ 0; at x = 0 any value in \[−1, 1\] is a valid subgradient. ReLU at 0 is handled the same way.*

**4. 单变量的梯度（完整推导） · Univariate gradient (full derivation)**

- 单个样本：L = ½(xw + b − y)²。令误差 e = xw + b − y。

  *One example: L = ½(xw + b − y)². Let the error be e = xw + b − y.*
- 链式法则：∂L/∂w = e·∂e/∂w = e·x；∂L/∂b = e·∂e/∂b = e。

  *Chain rule: ∂L/∂w = e·x and ∂L/∂b = e.*
- 对 m 个样本取平均即得 J 的梯度：

  *Averaging over the m examples gives the gradient of J:*

```latex
\frac{\partial J}{\partial w} = \frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m} e^{(i)} x^{(i)}, \qquad \frac{\partial J}{\partial b} = \frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m} e^{(i)}, \qquad e^{(i)} = x^{(i)}w + b - y^{(i)}
```

**5. 梯度下降怎么走（课件那张图） · How gradient descent moves (the slide figure)**

- 规则：w ← w − α·∂J/∂w。导数为负（在最低点左边）→ w 往右移；导数为正（在右边）→ 往左移。

  *Rule: w ← w − α·∂J/∂w. A negative derivative (left of the minimum) moves w right; a positive one moves it left.*
- α 太大：w 在最低点两侧来回跳；α 太小：要很多步才到。

  *Too large α: w jumps back and forth across the minimum. Too small: many steps are needed.*
- **算例**：J = w² − 2w + 1（c₁ = 1、c₂ = −2、c₃ = 1），J′ = 2w − 2，最低点 w = 1。从 w₀ = 0 出发：

  *Worked example: J = w² − 2w + 1, J′ = 2w − 2, minimum at w = 1, starting from w₀ = 0:*
  - α = 0.25：w = 0 → 0.5 → 0.75 → 0.875 → …，一直在左边，每步差距减半。

    *α = 0.25: 0 → 0.5 → 0.75 → 0.875, staying on the left and halving the gap each step.*
  - α = 0.75：w = 0 → 1.5 → 0.75 → 1.125 → …，左右来回跳着收敛（就是课件图里 w₀ 在左、w₁ 跳到右边、w₂ 又回到左边的情形）。

    *α = 0.75: 0 → 1.5 → 0.75 → 1.125, converging while hopping from side to side (the slide's w₀ left, w₁ right, w₂ left).*
- 完整训练循环：初始化 w（如 0.1 或随机数）→ 重复：算每个样本的预测 → 算平均损失 → 算 ∂J/∂w、∂J/∂b → 更新 w、b。

  *Full training loop: initialise w (e.g. 0.1 or random), then repeat: predict for each example → average loss → compute ∂J/∂w and ∂J/∂b → update w and b.*

**手算一步完整更新（闭卷典型题） · One full update by hand (typical exam question)**

数据两个样本 (x, y) = (1, 3)、(2, 5)；初始 w = 0、b = 0；α = 0.1。

*Two examples (x, y) = (1, 3) and (2, 5); start at w = 0, b = 0; α = 0.1.*

1. 预测：0、0。误差 e = −3、−5。

   *Predictions 0 and 0; errors e = −3 and −5.*
2. 损失：J = (1/2)·\[½·9 + ½·25\] = 8.5。

   *Loss J = (1/2)·\[½·9 + ½·25\] = 8.5.*
3. 梯度：∂J/∂w = (1/2)\[(−3)(1) + (−5)(2)\] = −6.5；∂J/∂b = (1/2)(−3 − 5) = −4。

   *Gradients: ∂J/∂w = −6.5 and ∂J/∂b = −4.*
4. 更新：w = 0 − 0.1·(−6.5) = 0.65；b = 0 − 0.1·(−4) = 0.4。

   *Update: w = 0.65, b = 0.4.*
5. 检查：新预测 1.05、1.7，误差 −1.95、−3.3，J = (1/2)\[½·3.8025 + ½·10.89\] ≈ 3.67，确实变小了。

   *Check: new predictions 1.05 and 1.7, errors −1.95 and −3.3, J ≈ 3.67 — it went down.*

**6. 多变量线性回归 · Multivariate linear regression**

- 房价预测：每套房子有多个特征（面积、楼层、位置、房龄、剩余租约……），排成列向量 x ∈ ℝⁿˣ¹，目标 y 是价格。

  *House prices: each house has several features (size, floor, location, age, remaining lease, …) stacked as a column vector x ∈ ℝⁿˣ¹; the target y is the price.*
- 模型：ỹ = wᵀx + b = w₁x₁ + w₂x₂ + … + wₙxₙ + b。wᵢ 可理解为第 i 个特征的重要程度。

  *Model: ỹ = wᵀx + b = w₁x₁ + … + wₙxₙ + b; wᵢ can be read as the importance of feature i.*
- **吸收偏置的技巧**：给 x 末尾补一个 1，给 w 末尾补一个 b，就有 ỹ = wᵀx，不用再单独写 b。之后的公式都用这个写法。

  *Bias trick: append 1 to x and b to w, so ỹ = wᵀx without a separate b. All later formulas use this form.*

```latex
\tilde y = \mathbf{w}^\top\mathbf{x} + b = \sum_{i=1}^{n} w_i x_i + b = \begin{pmatrix} w_1 & \cdots & w_n & b\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix} x_1 \\ \vdots \\ x_n \\ 1\end{pmatrix}
```

**单个样本的梯度 · Gradient for one example**

- 令 z = wᵀx − y（标量），J = ½z²。链式法则：∂J/∂w = (∂J/∂z)·(∂z/∂w) = z·x = (wᵀx − y)x。

  *Let z = wᵀx − y (a scalar) and J = ½z². By the chain rule ∂J/∂w = z·x = (wᵀx − y)x.*
- **形状检查**：z 是标量、x 是 n×1，结果是 n×1，和 w 一样 ✓。

  *Shape check: z is a scalar and x is n×1, so the result is n×1, matching w ✓.*
- **分母布局**（课上的约定）：标量对列向量求导，结果是同形状的列向量。常用结论：∂(wᵀx)/∂w = x；∂(‖w‖²)/∂w = 2w；∂(wᵀAw)/∂w = (A + Aᵀ)w。矩阵对矩阵求导太复杂，课上不用。

  *Denominator layout (the course convention): the derivative of a scalar with respect to a column vector is a column vector of the same shape. Useful results: ∂(wᵀx)/∂w = x; ∂‖w‖²/∂w = 2w; ∂(wᵀAw)/∂w = (A + Aᵀ)w. Matrix-by-matrix derivatives are not used.*

**7. 向量化：m 个样本一次算完 · Vectorisation: all m examples at once**

- 朴素做法：for 循环逐个样本累加损失和梯度，再除以 m。很慢。

  *Naive approach: loop over examples accumulating loss and gradient, then divide by m. Slow.*
- 把每个样本的特征放成矩阵 X 的一行（X 是 m×n；NumPy 按行存储，这样访问更快），目标排成列向量 y（m×1）。

  *Put each example as one row of X (m×n; NumPy is row-major, so this is faster) and the targets in a column vector y (m×1).*
- 预测 ỹ = Xw（m×1）；误差 u = ỹ − y；损失 J = (1/2m)uᵀu；梯度 ∂J/∂w = (1/m)Xᵀu。

  *Predictions ỹ = Xw (m×1); errors u = ỹ − y; loss J = (1/2m)uᵀu; gradient ∂J/∂w = (1/m)Xᵀu.*
- **形状检查**：Xᵀ 是 n×m、u 是 m×1，乘出来 n×1，和 w 一样 ✓。考试写完任何梯度，先做形状检查，最省事的查错办法。

  *Shape check: Xᵀ is n×m and u is m×1, giving n×1 like w ✓. After writing any gradient in the exam, check the shapes first — the cheapest way to catch mistakes.*

```latex
J(\mathbf{w}) = \frac{1}{2m}\,\|X\mathbf{w} - \mathbf{y}\|^2 = \frac{1}{2m}(X\mathbf{w}-\mathbf{y})^\top(X\mathbf{w}-\mathbf{y}), \qquad \frac{\partial J}{\partial \mathbf{w}} = \frac{1}{m}X^\top(X\mathbf{w} - \mathbf{y})
```

- **算例**（和上面的手算是同一题）：X = \[\[1, 1\], \[2, 1\]\]（第二列是补的 1），y = (3, 5)ᵀ，w = (0, 0)ᵀ。u = (−3, −5)ᵀ；Xᵀu = (1·(−3) + 2·(−5), (−3) + (−5)) = (−13, −8)；除以 m = 2 得 (−6.5, −4)，和逐样本算的一模一样 ✓。

  *Worked example (same data as above): X = \[\[1, 1\], \[2, 1\]\] (second column is the appended 1), y = (3, 5)ᵀ, w = 0. Then u = (−3, −5)ᵀ, Xᵀu = (−13, −8), and dividing by m = 2 gives (−6.5, −4), the same as the per-example calculation ✓.*

**8. 解析解（正规方程，作业 2） · Closed form (normal equation, Assignment 2)**

- 线性回归的损失是凸的，可以直接令梯度为 0：Xᵀ(Xw − y) = 0 ⇒ XᵀXw = Xᵀy ⇒ w\* = (XᵀX)⁻¹Xᵀy（要求 XᵀX 可逆）。

  *The linear-regression loss is convex, so set the gradient to zero: Xᵀ(Xw − y) = 0 ⇒ XᵀXw = Xᵀy ⇒ w∗ = (XᵀX)⁻¹Xᵀy (if XᵀX is invertible).*
- **算例**：上面两个点恰好在直线 y = 2x + 1 上。XᵀX = \[\[5, 3\], \[3, 2\]\]，Xᵀy = (13, 8)ᵀ。解 5a + 3b = 13、3a + 2b = 8 得 a = 2、b = 1，即 w = 2、b = 1 ✓。

  *Worked example: the two points lie on y = 2x + 1. XᵀX = \[\[5, 3\], \[3, 2\]\] and Xᵀy = (13, 8)ᵀ; solving gives w = 2, b = 1 ✓.*
- **Ridge（L2 正则）**：损失加 λ‖w‖²，解变成 (XᵀX + λI)⁻¹Xᵀy：永远可逆、权重被压小（λ 的具体系数随损失的缩放约定而变）。

  *Ridge (L2): add λ‖w‖²; the solution becomes (XᵀX + λI)⁻¹Xᵀy — always invertible, with shrunken weights (the exact factor on λ depends on how the loss is scaled).*
- **Lasso（L1 正则）**：加 λ‖w‖₁，没有闭式解；倾向于把一些权重压成恰好 0，相当于自动选特征。

  *Lasso (L1): add λ‖w‖₁; no closed form; it tends to set some weights exactly to zero, i.e. automatic feature selection.*

```latex
\mathbf{w}^* = (X^\top X)^{-1}X^\top\mathbf{y}, \qquad \mathbf{w}^*_{\text{ridge}} = (X^\top X + \lambda I)^{-1}X^\top\mathbf{y}
```

**9. PyTorch 实现要点（作业 2 tutorial） · PyTorch notes (Assignment 2 tutorial)**

- autograd 在前向时自动建计算图；调用 loss.backward() 自动算出 ∂L/∂w、∂L/∂b。

  *Autograd builds the computation graph during the forward pass; loss.backward() computes ∂L/∂w and ∂L/∂b.*
- 梯度默认**累加**到 .grad 里，所以每步要 optimizer.zero\_grad()，否则用的是历史梯度之和。

  *Gradients accumulate into .grad by default, so call optimizer.zero\_grad() every step; otherwise you use the sum of past gradients.*
- 手动更新参数要在计算图之外做（torch.no\_grad()）；用 optimizer.step() 则自动处理。

  *Manual parameter updates go outside the graph (torch.no\_grad()); optimizer.step() handles this for you.*

**10. 机器学习流程 · ML pipeline**：准备数据 → 提取特征 → 建模 → 选损失函数 → 训练 → 评估 → 部署。

*Prepare data → extract features → build the model → choose the loss → train → evaluate → deploy.*

**本讲易错点 · Common mistakes**

- 忘记除以 m（平均）。J 是平均损失，梯度也要除以 m。

  *Forgetting to divide by m: J is an average, so its gradient is divided by m too.*
- 把 x⁽ⁱ⁾ 当成 x 的 i 次方。

  *Reading x⁽ⁱ⁾ as x to the power i.*
- 形状不对：梯度必须和参数同形状；写成 Xu 而不是 Xᵀu 就会形状不匹配。

  *Wrong shapes: the gradient must have the parameter's shape; writing Xu instead of Xᵀu breaks it.*
- 更新是减去梯度，梯度为负时参数变大。

  *The update subtracts the gradient, so a negative gradient increases the parameter.*

### L5 感知机、反向传播、回归与分类 · Perceptrons, Backpropagation, Regression and Classification

**这一讲在干什么**：三件事。① 从一个神经元（感知机）堆成多层网络（MLP），并用链式法则（反向传播）求梯度；② 回归里的过拟合/欠拟合和数据划分；③ 分类：sigmoid + 二元交叉熵、softmax + 交叉熵。闭卷必考：数参数、手算反向传播、为什么分类不用 L2 损失、交叉熵梯度 p − y 的推导、显存估算。

*What this lecture is about: three things. (1) Stack single neurons (perceptrons) into a multi-layer network (MLP) and compute gradients with the chain rule (backpropagation). (2) Over/underfitting and data splitting in regression. (3) Classification with sigmoid + binary cross-entropy and softmax + cross-entropy. Always examined: counting parameters, backprop by hand, why classification avoids the L2 loss, deriving the p − y gradient, and memory estimates.*

**1. 感知机：一个神经元 · The perceptron: one neuron**

- 两步：先**加权求和** z = wᵀx + b，再过**激活函数** g(z)。原始感知机的激活是阶跃/符号函数：wᵀx > 0 输出 1，否则输出 0。

  *Two steps: a weighted sum z = wᵀx + b, then an activation function g(z). The original perceptron used a step/sign activation: output 1 if wᵀx > 0, else 0.*
- 如果激活函数是线性的（g(z) = z），感知机就是 L4 的线性回归。

  *With a linear activation (g(z) = z), a perceptron is exactly L4's linear regression.*
- 「世界上最小的感知机」：ŷ = wx，损失 L = ½(y − ŷ)²，导数 dL/dw = −(y − ŷ)x，更新 w ← w + η(y − ŷ)x。训练代码只有两行：算预测、更新 w。

  *"The world's smallest perceptron": ŷ = wx, L = ½(y − ŷ)², dL/dw = −(y − ŷ)x, update w ← w + η(y − ŷ)x. The training code is two lines: predict, update.*
- 导数的两种理解：① 曲线在一点的斜率；② 机器上的旋钮——把这个参数微微拧一下，输出（损失）会变多少。

  *Two views of a derivative: (1) the slope of a curve at a point; (2) a knob on a machine — how much the output (loss) changes when you turn this parameter slightly.*
- 损失函数由你选：L1、L2、零一损失、hinge 损失……不同任务合适的不同。

  *You choose the loss: L1, L2, zero-one, hinge, … each suits different tasks.*

```latex
z = \mathbf{w}^\top\mathbf{x} + b, \qquad \hat y = g(z), \qquad g_{\text{step}}(z) = \begin{cases} 1 & z > 0 \\ 0 & \text{otherwise}\end{cases}
```

**2. 多层感知机 MLP · Multi-layer perceptron**

- 神经网络 = 把一堆感知机连起来。输入层 → 若干**隐藏层** → 输出层。相邻两层的神经元两两相连，叫**全连接层**（fully connected layer）。

  *A neural network is many perceptrons connected: input layer → hidden layers → output layer. When every neuron in one layer connects to every neuron in the next, it is a fully connected layer.*
- **数参数（必考）**：3 个输入 → 4 个隐藏神经元 → 2 个输出。

  *Counting parameters (always examined): 3 inputs → 4 hidden neurons → 2 outputs.*
  - 神经元个数：4 + 2 = 6（**输入层不算神经元**，它只是数据）。

    *Neurons: 4 + 2 = 6 (the input layer is data, not neurons).*
  - 权重（连线）：3×4 + 4×2 = 20。

    *Weights (edges): 3×4 + 4×2 = 20.*
  - 偏置：每个神经元一个，共 6 个。

    *Biases: one per neuron, 6 in total.*
  - 可学习参数共 20 + 6 = 26。

    *Learnable parameters: 20 + 6 = 26.*
- 通用公式：第 l 层有 n\_l 个神经元，参数总数 = Σ (n\_{l−1}·n\_l + n\_l)。例：784 → 128 → 10 的网络：784×128 + 128 + 128×10 + 10 = 101,770。

  *General formula: with n\_l neurons in layer l, total = Σ(n\_{l−1}·n\_l + n\_l). Example: 784 → 128 → 10 has 784×128 + 128 + 128×10 + 10 = 101,770.*

```latex
\#\text{params} = \sum_{l=1}^{L}\bigl(n_{l-1}\, n_l + n_l\bigr)
```

**3. 训练一个网络：前向 → 损失 → 反向 → 更新 · Training: forward → loss → backward → update**

对每个样本（或每个 mini-batch）：

*For each sample (or mini-batch):*

1. **前向传播**：从输入一层层算到输出，得到预测。

   *Forward pass: compute layer by layer from input to output to get the prediction.*
2. **算损失**：预测和真值差多少。

   *Compute the loss: how far the prediction is from the truth.*
3. **反向传播**：用链式法则从输出往输入方向，算每个参数的偏导数。「反向」的意思是：靠近输出处算好的导数会被靠近输入处重复使用。

   *Backward pass: apply the chain rule from output back to input to get each parameter's partial derivative. "Back" because derivatives computed near the output are reused by the layers nearer the input.*
4. **梯度更新**：所有参数同时 w ← w − α·∂L/∂w。

   *Gradient update: every parameter at once, w ← w − α·∂L/∂w.*

一个 4 层、每层一个权重的网络：a₁ = f₁(w₁x)，a₂ = f₂(w₂a₁)，a₃ = f₃(w₃a₂)，ŷ = f₄(w₄a₃)。a₁、a₂、a₃ 叫**激活值**。整个网络就是一个长式子，x、y 已知，w 未知。链式法则：

*A four-layer network with one weight per layer: a₁ = f₁(w₁x), a₂ = f₂(w₂a₁), a₃ = f₃(w₃a₂), ŷ = f₄(w₄a₃). The a's are activations. The network is one long expression with x and y known and the w's unknown. Chain rule:*

```latex
\frac{\partial L}{\partial w_1} = \frac{\partial L}{\partial \hat y}\cdot\frac{\partial \hat y}{\partial a_3}\cdot\frac{\partial a_3}{\partial a_2}\cdot\frac{\partial a_2}{\partial a_1}\cdot\frac{\partial a_1}{\partial w_1}
```

算 ∂L/∂w₄ 时已经算过 ∂L/∂ŷ，算 ∂L/∂w₃ 时又用到它和 ∂ŷ/∂a₃……从后往前算，每一段只算一次。

*Computing ∂L/∂w₄ already gives ∂L/∂ŷ; ∂L/∂w₃ reuses it with ∂ŷ/∂a₃, and so on. Working backwards, each factor is computed once.*

**手算反向传播（闭卷典型题） · Backprop by hand (typical exam question)**

网络：z = w₁x，h = σ(z)，ŷ = w₂h，L = ½(ŷ − y)²。已知 x = 1、y = 1、w₁ = 0、w₂ = 1、α = 1。

*Network: z = w₁x, h = σ(z), ŷ = w₂h, L = ½(ŷ − y)², with x = 1, y = 1, w₁ = 0, w₂ = 1, α = 1.*

1. 前向：z = 0，h = σ(0) = 0.5，ŷ = 0.5，L = ½(0.5 − 1)² = 0.125。

   *Forward: z = 0, h = 0.5, ŷ = 0.5, L = 0.125.*
2. ∂L/∂ŷ = ŷ − y = −0.5。

   *∂L/∂ŷ = ŷ − y = −0.5.*
3. ∂L/∂w₂ = ∂L/∂ŷ · h = −0.5 × 0.5 = −0.25。

   *∂L/∂w₂ = −0.5 × 0.5 = −0.25.*
4. ∂L/∂h = ∂L/∂ŷ · w₂ = −0.5 × 1 = −0.5（用的是**更新前**的 w₂）。

   *∂L/∂h = −0.5 × 1 = −0.5, using w₂ before the update.*
5. ∂L/∂z = ∂L/∂h · σ′(z) = −0.5 × σ(0)(1 − σ(0)) = −0.5 × 0.25 = −0.125。

   *∂L/∂z = −0.5 × σ′(0) = −0.5 × 0.25 = −0.125.*
6. ∂L/∂w₁ = ∂L/∂z · x = −0.125。

   *∂L/∂w₁ = −0.125 × 1 = −0.125.*
7. 更新：w₂ = 1 + 0.25 = 1.25，w₁ = 0 + 0.125 = 0.125。

   *Update: w₂ = 1.25, w₁ = 0.125.*
8. 检查：新的前向 h = σ(0.125) ≈ 0.531，ŷ ≈ 0.664，L ≈ 0.056，损失变小了 ✓。

   *Check: new forward pass gives h ≈ 0.531, ŷ ≈ 0.664, L ≈ 0.056 — lower ✓.*

**课上的几个问答 · Questions from class**

- 问：w₁、w₂ 的导数是同时算还是先后算，会影响结果吗？——不会。在「梯度更新」之前所有参数都没变，先算哪个都一样。（上面第 4 步用的就是旧的 w₂。）

  *Q: Does computing the derivatives of w₁ and w₂ concurrently or sequentially change the result? — No. Nothing changes before the gradient update, so the order does not matter (step 4 above uses the old w₂).*
- 问：每个样本经过网络时激活值不同吗？——不同。权重是共享的，但输入不同，而且非线性激活会放大差异。

  *Q: Do different samples produce different activations? — Yes. The weights are shared but the inputs differ, and non-linear activations amplify the differences.*
- 问：前向传播后能删掉激活值吗？——不能。反向传播的链式法则要用到它们（上例第 3 步用到 h，第 5 步用到 z）；重新计算又太贵。这就是训练比推理占显存的原因之一。

  *Q: Can activations be deleted after the forward pass? — No. Backprop's chain rule needs them (step 3 uses h, step 5 uses z), and recomputing them is expensive. This is one reason training needs more memory than inference.*

**4. 回归：多项式回归与过拟合/欠拟合 · Polynomial regression, over- and underfitting**

- 多项式回归：ỹ = xw₁ + x²w₂ + … + xᴹw\_M + b。训练数据来自 y = f(x) + 随机噪声。次数 M 是超参数，要挑。

  *Polynomial regression: ỹ = xw₁ + x²w₂ + … + xᴹw\_M + b, with data from y = f(x) + noise. The degree M is a hyperparameter to choose.*
- **欠拟合 = 高偏差**：模型容量太小（比如 M = 0，ỹ = b；或 M = 1，一条直线），连训练数据都拟合不好。偏差（bias）的定义：算法因为没有利用数据里足够的信息，而「稳定地学错」的倾向。

  *Underfitting = high bias: the model's capacity is too small (M = 0 or M = 1) to fit even the training data. Bias is the tendency to consistently learn the wrong thing by not using enough of the information in the data.*
- **过拟合 = 高方差**：模型容量太大（比如 M = 9 去拟合 10 个点），穿过每个训练点、曲线乱晃，训练误差几乎为 0，测试误差很大。方差（variance）的定义：换一份同分布的训练数据，学出来的参数会变多少。

  *Overfitting = high variance: capacity too large (M = 9 for 10 points) — the curve passes through every training point and wiggles, training error is near 0 and test error is large. Variance is how much the learned parameters would change with a different training set from the same source.*
- **好模型**：容量刚好（如 M = 3），在欠拟合和过拟合、偏差和方差之间取得平衡。

  *A good model has the right capacity (e.g. M = 3), balancing under- and overfitting, bias and variance.*
- 问：能不能同时低偏差、低方差？——课件的答案：不能，两者由同一个因素（模型容量）控制，方向相反。

  *Q: Can we have low bias and low variance together? — The slides say no: both are controlled by the same factor, model capacity, in opposite directions.*
- 实用策略：**先用更大的模型压低偏差（避免欠拟合），再用正则化压低方差（避免过拟合）**。

  *Practical recipe: first train a bigger model to reduce bias (avoid underfitting), then regularise to reduce variance (avoid overfitting).*

| · | 欠拟合 · Underfitting | 过拟合 · Overfitting |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又叫 · Also called | 高偏差 · high bias | 高方差 · high variance |
| 模型容量 · Capacity | 太小 · too small | 太大 · too large |
| 训练误差 · Training error | 高 · high | 很低 · very low |
| 测试误差 · Test error | 高 · high | 高 · high |
| 怎么办 · Fix | 加大模型、加特征 · bigger model, more features | 正则化、更多数据、早停 · regularisation, more data, early stopping |

**5. 超参数与数据划分 · Hyperparameters and data splits**

- **参数**（如 w、b）由训练学出；**超参数**由人预先定：多项式次数、学习率、层数。调超参数叫超参数调优或模型选择。

  *Parameters (w, b) are learned; hyperparameters (polynomial degree, learning rate, number of layers) are set by people. Tuning them is hyperparameter tuning or model selection.*
- **铁律**：绝不在测试数据上训练或调参。测试数据只用来报告最终的无偏成绩——学生考前不能看考题和答案。

  *Golden rule: never train or tune on test data. Test data only reports the final unbiased performance — students cannot see the exam before the exam.*
- 三个集合的分工：训练集学参数；验证集选超参数；测试集报告成绩。

  *Roles: training set learns parameters; validation set selects hyperparameters; test set reports the score.*
- 怎么切：数据多时按固定比例（80:20、90:10、95:5）；数据少时用 **K 折交叉验证**：把训练数据分成 K 份，轮流用 K − 1 份训练、1 份验证，取 K 次验证结果的平均。

  *How to split: with plenty of data, a fixed ratio (80:20, 90:10, 95:5); with little data, K-fold cross-validation — split the training data into K parts, train on K − 1 and validate on the remaining one in turn, and average the K validation scores.*
- **算例**（课件原例）：

  *Worked example (from the slides):*

| 多项式次数 · Degree | 训练误差 · Training error | 验证误差 · Validation error |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | 1.2 | 1.3 |
| 2 | 0.5 | 0.3 |
| 9 | 0.1 | 0.4 |

应该选次数 2：看验证误差，不看训练误差（次数 9 训练误差最小但已经过拟合）。选定后再在测试集上报告一次成绩。

*Choose degree 2: decide by validation error, not training error (degree 9 has the lowest training error but overfits). Then report the test score once.*

- 三种场景：① 真实应用：把全部数据分成训练和验证，希望验证数据能代表部署后遇到的数据；② Kaggle 类比赛：把训练数据分成训练和验证，测试成绩由主办方在私有测试集上算；③ 研究：先切出测试集（5%、10% 或 20%），再把剩下的分成训练和验证。

  *Three settings: (1) real applications — split all data into training and validation, hoping validation represents deployment data; (2) Kaggle-style challenges — split the training data into training and validation; organisers score on a private test set; (3) research — hold out a test set first (5%, 10% or 20%), then split the rest into training and validation.*
- 作业 3 的要点：标准化用的均值和标准差**只能在训练集上算**，再拿去变换验证集和测试集；用了测试集的统计量就是数据泄露。

  *Assignment 3 point: compute normalisation means and standard deviations on the training set only, then apply them to validation and test sets; using test statistics is data leakage.*

**6. 分类：从回归到分类 · From regression to classification**

- 回归预测连续的数（明天气温多少度）；分类预测有限个类别之一（明天晴吗？是晴、多云还是雨？）。分类标签常常是从连续值定义出来的（下多少毫米雨算「雨天」？）。

  *Regression predicts a continuous quantity (tomorrow's temperature); classification predicts one of a finite set of labels (sunny? sunny, cloudy or rainy?). Class labels are often derived from continuous values (how many mm of rain makes a rainy day?).*
- **多类单标签**：只能属于一类，标签写成 one-hot 向量，如周一晴 = (1, 0, 0)。

  *Multi-class single-label: exactly one class, written as a one-hot vector, e.g. sunny Monday = (1, 0, 0).*
- **多类多标签**：可以同时属于多类，如周一「晴且多云」= (1, 0, 1)。

  *Multi-class multi-label: several classes at once, e.g. "sunny and cloudy" Monday = (1, 0, 1).*
- 最朴素的办法：给回归输出加阈值（感知机），wᵀx + b ≥ c 就输出 1。阈值 c 可以并进 b，变成 wᵀx + (b − c) ≥ 0。

  *Simplest approach: threshold the regression output (a perceptron), output 1 if wᵀx + b ≥ c. The threshold c can be merged into the bias: wᵀx + (b − c) ≥ 0.*
- **但阶跃函数不能用梯度下降训练**：它只是分段可导，而每一段都是常数，导数处处为 0，什么也学不到。

  *But a step function cannot be trained by gradient descent: it is only piecewise differentiable, each piece is constant, the derivative is zero everywhere, and nothing is learned.*

**7. 逻辑回归：sigmoid + 二元交叉熵 · Logistic regression: sigmoid + binary cross-entropy**

- **sigmoid（逻辑函数）**：σ(z) = 1/(1 + e⁻ᶻ)，输出在 (0, 1) 之间，可以解释成「标签为 1 的概率」。它光滑可导，导数 σ′ = σ(1 − σ)，在 z = 0 处最大为 0.25。

  *Sigmoid (logistic function) σ(z) = 1/(1 + e⁻ᶻ): outputs in (0, 1), read as the probability that the label is 1. It is smooth, with derivative σ′ = σ(1 − σ), largest (0.25) at z = 0.*
- 常用值：σ(0) = 0.5；σ(2) ≈ 0.88；σ(−2) ≈ 0.12；σ(10) ≈ 0.99995。

  *Handy values: σ(0) = 0.5, σ(2) ≈ 0.88, σ(−2) ≈ 0.12, σ(10) ≈ 0.99995.*

```latex
\sigma(z) = \frac{1}{1+e^{-z}}, \qquad \sigma'(z) = \sigma(z)\bigl(1-\sigma(z)\bigr), \qquad p = \sigma(\mathbf{w}^\top\mathbf{x} + b)
```

**为什么不用 L2 损失：梯度消失 · Why not the L2 loss: vanishing gradients**

- 若用 L = ½(p − y)²，则 ∂L/∂z = (p − y)·p(1 − p)。

  *With L = ½(p − y)², ∂L/∂z = (p − y)·p(1 − p).*
- 当 z 很大或很小时，p 贴近 1 或 0，sigmoid 曲线很平，p(1 − p) ≈ 0，梯度几乎为 0——**即使预测是错的**。训练卡住，这叫梯度消失。曲线越平，梯度越小。

  *When z is very large or very small, p is near 1 or 0, the sigmoid is flat, p(1 − p) ≈ 0 and the gradient vanishes — even when the prediction is wrong. Training gets stuck: the vanishing-gradient problem. The flatter the curve, the smaller the gradient.*
- 算例：z = −10、y = 1（模型自信地答错）。p ≈ 4.5 × 10⁻⁵。L2 的梯度 ≈ (−1)·(4.5 × 10⁻⁵)·1 ≈ −4.5 × 10⁻⁵，几乎不动；交叉熵的梯度 p − y ≈ −1，大力纠正。

  *Example: z = −10, y = 1 (confidently wrong). p ≈ 4.5 × 10⁻⁵. The L2 gradient is about −4.5 × 10⁻⁵ — almost no movement; the cross-entropy gradient p − y ≈ −1 corrects strongly.*

**二元交叉熵（BCE）从哪来 · Where binary cross-entropy comes from**

1. 分类器「猜对」的概率：y = 1 时是 p，y = 0 时是 1 − p。合成一个式子：P(猜对) = p^y·(1 − p)^(1−y)。

   *The probability the classifier is correct: p when y = 1 and 1 − p when y = 0, i.e. P(correct) = p^y·(1 − p)^(1−y).*
2. 取对数：log P(猜对) = y log p + (1 − y) log(1 − p)。

   *Take logs: y log p + (1 − y) log(1 − p).*
3. 我们要最大化猜对的概率，等价于最小化负对数似然，就得到 BCE。所以交叉熵损失 = 最大化分类器的对数似然。

   *Maximising the probability of being correct is minimising the negative log-likelihood, which is BCE. So the cross-entropy loss maximises the classifier's log-likelihood.*
4. 拆开看：y = 1 时只剩 −log p（p 越接近 1 损失越小）；y = 0 时只剩 −log(1 − p)。

   *Case by case: y = 1 leaves −log p (smaller as p → 1); y = 0 leaves −log(1 − p).*

- 数值：y = 1、p = 0.9 → L = 0.105；p = 0.5 → 0.693；p = 0.1 → 2.303。越自信地答错，罚得越重。

  *Numbers: with y = 1, p = 0.9 gives 0.105, p = 0.5 gives 0.693, p = 0.1 gives 2.303. Confident mistakes are punished hardest.*

```latex
L_{\mathrm{BCE}}(p, y) = -\bigl[\,y\log p + (1-y)\log(1-p)\,\bigr]
```

**BCE 对 z 的梯度恰好是 p − y（必会推导） · The BCE gradient with respect to z is exactly p − y (must derive)**

- ∂L/∂p = −y/p + (1 − y)/(1 − p)；∂p/∂z = p(1 − p)。

  *∂L/∂p = −y/p + (1 − y)/(1 − p) and ∂p/∂z = p(1 − p).*
- 相乘：−y(1 − p) + (1 − y)p = −y + yp + p − yp = p − y。

  *Multiply: −y(1 − p) + (1 − y)p = p − y.*
- sigmoid 导数里的 p(1 − p) 被约掉了，所以不会梯度消失。对权重：∂L/∂w = (p − y)x，∂L/∂b = p − y。

  *The p(1 − p) from the sigmoid cancels, so the gradient does not vanish. For the weights: ∂L/∂w = (p − y)x and ∂L/∂b = p − y.*

```latex
\frac{\partial L}{\partial z} = \Bigl(-\frac{y}{p} + \frac{1-y}{1-p}\Bigr)\,p(1-p) = p - y, \qquad \frac{\partial L}{\partial \mathbf{w}} = (p-y)\,\mathbf{x}
```

**8. 多类多标签：每个类一个独立的 sigmoid · Multi-label: one independent sigmoid per class**

- 假设在给定输入后，属于各类是相互独立的。每个类 i 做一个二分类：pᵢ = σ(wᵢᵀx + bᵢ)，损失是各类 BCE 之和。

  *Assume class memberships are independent given the input. Each class i is a binary problem, pᵢ = σ(wᵢᵀx + bᵢ), and the loss is the sum of the per-class BCEs.*
- 向量化：z = Wx + b（W 是 K×n），p = σ(z) 逐元素。各个 pᵢ 加起来不一定等于 1。

  *Vectorised: z = Wx + b (W is K×n), p = σ(z) element-wise; the pᵢ need not sum to 1.*
- 例：新加坡游泳队在蛙泳、蝶泳、仰泳、自由泳各拿不拿金牌？输出 (1, 0, 1, 1)。

  *Example: does Singapore's swimming team win gold in breaststroke, butterfly, backstroke, freestyle? Output (1, 0, 1, 1).*

**9. 多类单标签：softmax + 交叉熵 · Multi-class single-label: softmax + cross-entropy**

- 类别互斥：p(晴) 大，p(雨)、p(云) 就该小，而且三者之和应为 1。独立 sigmoid 做不到，所以让每个 pᵢ 都依赖全部 zⱼ。

  *Classes are exclusive: if p(sunny) is large, p(rainy) and p(cloudy) must be small, and the three should sum to 1. Independent sigmoids cannot enforce this, so each pᵢ depends on all zⱼ.*
- **softmax**：pᵢ = e^(zᵢ)/Σⱼ e^(zⱼ)。z 叫 **logit**（未归一化的分数）。所有 pᵢ > 0 且和为 1。又叫多项式逻辑回归。

  *Softmax: pᵢ = e^(zᵢ)/Σⱼ e^(zⱼ). The z's are logits (unnormalised scores); all pᵢ > 0 and they sum to 1. Also called multinomial logistic regression.*
- 预测：取概率最大的类（argmax，「赢者通吃」），输出 one-hot。课件例：六个概率 0.166, 0.166, 0.166, 0.166, 0.17, 0.166，第 5 个只大一点点，预测也是 (0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0)。

  *Prediction: the class with the largest probability (argmax, "winner takes all"), output as one-hot. Slide example: probabilities 0.166, 0.166, 0.166, 0.166, 0.17, 0.166 — the fifth is barely larger, yet the prediction is (0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0).*
- **交叉熵损失**：L = −Σ yᵢ log pᵢ。因为 y 是 one-hot，就是 −log p\_正确类。

  *Cross-entropy loss L = −Σ yᵢ log pᵢ; with one-hot y it is −log of the correct class's probability.*
- **算例**：z = (2, 1, 0)。e^z = (7.389, 2.718, 1)，和 11.107，p = (0.665, 0.245, 0.090)。若正确类是第 1 类：L = −ln 0.665 = 0.408；梯度 p − y = (−0.335, 0.245, 0.090)——正确类的 logit 被往上推，其他的被往下压。

  *Worked example: z = (2, 1, 0) gives e^z = (7.389, 2.718, 1), sum 11.107, p = (0.665, 0.245, 0.090). If class 1 is correct, L = −ln 0.665 = 0.408 and the gradient p − y = (−0.335, 0.245, 0.090): the correct logit is pushed up and the others down.*

```latex
p_i = \mathrm{softmax}(\mathbf{z})_i = \frac{e^{z_i}}{\sum_{j=1}^{K} e^{z_j}}, \qquad L = -\sum_{i=1}^{K} y_i\log p_i
```

**softmax 交叉熵的梯度（推导） · Gradient of softmax cross-entropy (derivation)**

1. softmax 的导数（用商法则）：i = j 时 ∂pᵢ/∂zᵢ = pᵢ(1 − pᵢ)；i ≠ j 时 ∂pᵢ/∂zⱼ = −pᵢpⱼ。合写 ∂pᵢ/∂zⱼ = pᵢ(δᵢⱼ − pⱼ)，δᵢⱼ 在 i = j 时为 1，否则为 0。

   *Softmax derivative (quotient rule): ∂pᵢ/∂zᵢ = pᵢ(1 − pᵢ); for i ≠ j, ∂pᵢ/∂zⱼ = −pᵢpⱼ. Together, ∂pᵢ/∂zⱼ = pᵢ(δᵢⱼ − pⱼ), where δᵢⱼ is 1 if i = j and 0 otherwise.*
2. ∂L/∂zⱼ = −Σᵢ (yᵢ/pᵢ)·pᵢ(δᵢⱼ − pⱼ) = −yⱼ + pⱼΣᵢyᵢ = pⱼ − yⱼ（因为 Σyᵢ = 1）。

   *∂L/∂zⱼ = −Σᵢ (yᵢ/pᵢ)·pᵢ(δᵢⱼ − pⱼ) = −yⱼ + pⱼΣyᵢ = pⱼ − yⱼ, since Σyᵢ = 1.*
3. 与二元情形一样干净：**梯度 = 预测概率 − 真实标签**。对权重矩阵 W：∂L/∂W = (p − y)xᵀ。

   *As clean as the binary case: gradient = predicted probabilities − true labels. For the weight matrix, ∂L/∂W = (p − y)xᵀ.*

```latex
\frac{\partial p_i}{\partial z_j} = p_i(\delta_{ij} - p_j), \qquad \frac{\partial L}{\partial z_j} = p_j - y_j
```

| · | 多类单标签 · Single-label multi-class | 多类多标签 · Multi-label |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 例子 · Example | 百米金牌归哪国 · who wins the 100 m gold: (0, 0, 0, 1, 0) | 各泳姿拿不拿金牌 · gold in each stroke: (1, 0, 1, 1) |
| 输出层 · Output layer | softmax，概率和为 1 · softmax, sums to 1 | 每个类各自 sigmoid · one sigmoid per class |
| 损失 · Loss | −Σ yᵢ log pᵢ | 各类 BCE 之和 · sum of per-class BCE |
| 预测 · Prediction | argmax | 每类 pᵢ > 0.5 就标 1 · 1 where pᵢ > 0.5 |

**10. 显存估算 Quiz（L5、L6 都考） · Memory-estimate quiz (L5 and L6)**

题目：用梯度下降训练 300B（3000 亿）参数的模型，需要多少内存？1 GB = 10⁹ 字节；单精度（fp32）每个参数 4 字节，双精度（fp64）8 字节；激活值不超过参数大小；一个样本占 1 GB。

*Problem: how much memory to train a 300-billion-parameter model with gradient descent? 1 GB = 10⁹ bytes; single precision (fp32) is 4 bytes per parameter, double (fp64) 8 bytes; activations are no larger than the parameters; one sample takes 1 GB.*

- 要存四样：参数、梯度（和参数同形状，所以同样大）、激活值（反向传播要用）、输入样本。
